### Fixed

- **loopzero-cloud#37 reopened cenário 4 — second root cause: EnvironmentLister project-agnostic shallow check silently swallows new-project deploys against existing tenants (no Live URL)** — Plan `m37-tenant-cr-project-sync-canonical-fix` v1.0 (closes #37 cenário 4 BUILD → RELEASE → URL). Sprint 8 fixed the engine watcher namespace-scoped LIST (correct, LIVE). The QA Red Team re-test caught a SECOND root cause: `services.EnvironmentLister.HasEnvironment(ctx, tenantID, envName)` interface was project-agnostic per M2 v1.2 NOTE comment, so a `loopzero deploy` for a NEW project (e.g. `loopzerokit-example`) against a tenant that ALREADY had the same env name (`preview`) on a DIFFERENT project (`qa-redteam-dogfood`) returned exists=true on env-name match alone → `EnsureEnvironment` returned early WITHOUT calling `AddEnvironment` → Tenant CR `spec.environments[]` never gained the `(loopzerokit-example, preview)` tuple → tenant-controller never extended AppProject `<tenant>.spec.destinations[].namespace` to include `loopzero-<tenant>-loopzerokit-example-preview` → ArgoCD legitimately rejected every sync with `namespace "<...>" is not permitted in project '<tenant>'` → `release.status=FAILED, sync_status=unknown, active_release=null, url=""` after 5min retrigger-loop. Classic stale-helper post-architecture-refactor (`feedback_stale_helper_post_architecture_refactor_2026_06_18`). Fix: **T1.1** extended `EnvironmentLister.HasEnvironment` signature from `(ctx, tenantID, envName)` to `(ctx, tenantID, project, envName)` matching the canonical M2 v1.2 (tenantID, project, envName) tuple. Updated sole production adapter `envManagerAdapter` + 2 call-sites (initial check + poll loop) in `EnsureEnvironment` + test fakes. **T2.1** replaced the main.go closure adapter at line ~270-281 from name-only loop (`envs[i].Name == envName`) to tuple-aware filter (`envs[i].Name == envName && envs[i].Project == project`). Test footprint (all GREEN under `go test -race`): new `TestEnsureEnvironment_DifferentProjectSameEnvName_TriggersAddEnvironment` (the m37 RED test — pre-m37 fail because lister returned exists=true on env-name match across projects; post-m37 pass because the new tuple-aware closure returns exists=false → AddEnvironment fires with the new project), new `TestEnsureEnvironment_SameProjectSameEnvName_IsNoOp` (negative-direction guard — same tuple still no-ops, no double k8s round-trips), new `TestEnsureEnvironment_LegacyEmptyProject_HonoredOnShallowCheck` (ADR D2 regression-protector — `project=""` legacy production tuples still honored). Plus existing M2 v1.2 propagation tests + readiness-checker tests preserved. **T3.1+T3.2+T3.3** canonical 3-script triplet: `infra/tests/e2e/dev-public-deploy-live-url_test.sh` (LIVE 4 scenarios A-D covering Tenant CR tuple extension + AppProject destinations + release ACTIVE with URL + HTTP 200..399 probe, OPERATOR HARD STOP per cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate.md, 30min CLI ceiling honored) + `infra/tests/check-dev-public-deploy-live-url-syntax_test.sh` (hermetic 27/27 PASS) + `archive/canonical-tests-v1/scripts/local/dogfood-deploy-live-url.sh` (4-suite aggregate). Per the INQUEBRÁVEL feedback_issue_fix_requires_failing_e2e_test_2026_06_25: cenário-4 LIVE GREEN is the close-#37 contract. Plan file: `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m37-tenant-cr-project-sync-canonical-fix-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE).

- **loopzero-cloud#37 build SUCCEEDED mas release fica em SYNC_FAILED, `active_release: null`, sem Live URL (cenário 4 do handoff Sprint 5 não fecha)** — Sprint 8 plan `qa-sprint8-cleanup-2026-06-25` v1.0 (closes #37 BLOCKER). Root cause LIVE-traced via loopzero-api logs: `api/cmd/setup_server.go:570` instanciava `NewK8sWorkflowNodeSource(dyn, "", ...)` (empty namespace = cluster-scope LIST). Sprint 7 RBAC fix concedeu permissão `[create get list watch delete]` em `workflows.argoproj.io` para SA `argo/loopzero-workflow-submitter` mas SOMENTE namespace-scope (Role + RoleBinding, não ClusterRole). Resultado: o argo step watcher poll-loop 403-eava a cada 2s → reconcile loop não enxergava workflow status → release ficava `SYNC_FAILED` para sempre. Fix: `NewK8sWorkflowNodeSource(dyn, "argo", ...)` — namespace-scoped LIST que casa com a RBAC namespace-scoped que Sprint 7 já estabeleceu. **TODOS os workflows do LoopZero vivem na `argo` namespace** (criados por `workflow_submitter.go`); o comentário pré-Sprint-8 sobre "per-tenant namespaces" estava incorreto — só os BUILD PODS rodam per-tenant, não os Workflow CRs. KISS + least privilege. LIVE-validated: `kubectl --context k3d-loopzero-build -n argo get workflows.argoproj.io --as=system:serviceaccount:argo:loopzero-workflow-submitter` retorna a lista de workflows com sucesso; loopzero-api logs mostram ZERO 403s em 60s+ pós-rollout (pré-fix: storm contínuo a cada 2s). GHCR digest: `sha256:a7ad23dc4e203965bd2b15e8354f36950e3957dc687231c2272ae56dfd04d0e7`.

- **loopzero-cloud#38 DEPLOY_TIMEOUT do CLI (~600s) menor que build real (615s), deploy bem-sucedido reporta falha** — Sprint 8 plan `qa-sprint8-cleanup-2026-06-25` v1.0 (closes #38 MAJOR). Root cause: `loopzero-cli/src/config/constants.ts:118` definia `DEPLOY_TIMEOUT_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000` (10min). Build canonical do `loopzerokit-example` observado pelo QA Red Team durou 615s (10min15s) — apenas 15s acima do default — produzindo `DEPLOY_TIMEOUT` em deploy que de fato **SUCEDEU** logo depois. Fix: bump default 10min → 30min. Cold-start cosign signing + ArgoCD sync + workload pull podem empurrar canonical builds além de 10min; 30min cobre >99% dos casos sem permitir esperas runaway. Operadores podem overridar via `LOOPZERO_DEPLOY_TIMEOUT` env. O activity-based per-event timeout em `deploy.ts:557-572` continua resetando a cada SSE event recebido, então o ceiling de 30min só dispara quando o build em si stall sem progresso — semântica intencional preservada. 2 novos test cases em `tests/unit/config/constants.test.ts` (default = 30min + assertGreaterThanOrEqual canonical 615s); 20/20 testes GREEN.

### Security

- **loopzero-cloud#35 mensagens de erro de deploy vazam stack interna (Argo/ArgoCD/k8s SA/git-bundle/temp paths) a tenant comum (info-disclosure CWE-209)** — Sprint 7 plan `qa-sprint7-cleanup-2026-06-25` v1.0 (closes #35). A política Sprint 5/6 "preservar a causa real **verbatim** na mensagem do usuário" trocou um problema por outro: a causa interna passou a vazar ao cliente, expondo a stack de orquestração (Argo Workflows, ArgoCD, k8s ServiceAccount/namespace, git-bundle, paths temporários do servidor). Tenant `owner` lendo `loopzero deploy --json` mapeava a arquitetura inteira. Fix: extended `scrubPatterns` em `api/internal/source/pushbundle_error.go` com 11 novos padrões cobrindo `argoproj.io`, `argo: create workflow` (forma colon-prefixada que apareceu no `#36`), `argocd`, `argo workflows`, `system:serviceaccount:<ns>:<name>` (chain completa), `serviceaccount`, `namespace "<ns>"`, `namespace` standalone, `/tmp/loopzero-<slug>/<path>`, `git bundle`, `bundle`. Padrões `\bargo\b` standalone deliberadamente NÃO usados pois `\b` trata `-` como word-boundary (matchearia `argo` dentro de "my-argo-test" project name); usa-se em vez disso colon-suffix + word-form-específica patterns. Sprint 5 negative-direction guards (github.com / gitlab.com NOT touched) preservados. Test footprint: 8 novos sub-cases em `TestPushBundleError_ScrubsInternalTerms` (6 positive scrub + 2 negative — `my-argo-test` survives + `github.com` Sprint 5 regression). LIVE-PROOF: GHCR `ghcr.io/getloopscale/loopzero-api:develop` digest `sha256:0b66d29cce305fd34386ac3a7efe2e51df864103ea87b8d83395d1e755da8ef4` rolled out (4/4 pods).

### Fixed

- **loopzero-cloud#36 BUILD_SUBMIT_FAILED: 'argo: create workflow: Unauthorized' (build não enfileira, deploy não chega a Live URL)** — Sprint 7 plan `qa-sprint7-cleanup-2026-06-25` v1.0 (closes #36 BLOCKER). Root cause: `infra/scripts/local/regen-build-runtime-kubeconfigs.py:68-73` mintava token de `kube-system/default` SA para TODOS os clusters — mas o build cluster's Argo Workflows RBAC liga `workflows.argoproj.io: create` ao SA `argo/loopzero-workflow-submitter` (que já existe com Role + RoleBinding). O build kubeconfig em mgmt cluster carregava token errado → toda submissão de workflow retornava `Unauthorized` → build nunca enfileirava → `loopzero status: last_build: null, url: ""`. Fix: `CLUSTER_MAP` estendido com `(sa_namespace, sa_name)` tupla per cluster; build → `("argo", "loopzero-workflow-submitter")`; runtime + runtime2 mantém `("kube-system", "default")` (sem Argo Workloads pra submeter lá). `mint_token` parametrizado. LIVE-validated: `python3 infra/scripts/local/regen-build-runtime-kubeconfigs.py --only build --verify` aplicado contra dev-public — JWT sub claim agora reads `system:serviceaccount:argo:loopzero-workflow-submitter`; `kubectl auth can-i create workflows.argoproj.io -n argo --as=system:serviceaccount:argo:loopzero-workflow-submitter` retorna `yes`. Per NOVA INQUEBRÁVEL feedback (sponsor 2026-06-25 `feedback_issue_fix_requires_failing_e2e_test_2026_06_25`): E2E LIVE script `infra/tests/e2e/dev-public-build-rbac_test.sh` (4 cenários A-D) authored ANTES do fix e RED-confirmed against pre-fix engine, GREEN post-fix.

- **loopzero-cloud#34 engine.API INTERNAL_ERROR catchall no trigger do build (`loopzero deploy` cria projeto mas build não enfileira, "Something went wrong. Try again, or check `loopzero status` for details" não-acionável)** — Sprint 6 plan `qa-sprint6-cleanup-2026-06-25` v1.0 (closes #34). Root cause: `api/internal/routes/builds.go::mapBuildError` defaulted every non-`*LoopZeroError` to `loopzeroerrors.ErrInternalError`; `BuildOrchestrator.SubmitBuild` returned wrapped untyped errors at 7 sites via `fmt.Errorf("submit build: <op>: %w", err)` — none typed, all falling through to the catchall. Structurally identical to the #33 anti-pattern Sprint 5 closed at the source-upload surface. Fix: applies Sprint 5 canonical pattern (flyctl wrap-site discrimination + Vercel verbatim cause composition + Go `Unwrap()` chain + EC-3 sentinel preservation) to the build-trigger surface. **T1.1** added `SubmitBuildErrorReason` enum with 5 reasons (`ReasonBuildSubmitWorkflowFailed=1`, `ReasonBuildSubmitProjectEnsureFailed=2`, `ReasonBuildSubmitDBWriteFailed=3`, `ReasonBuildSubmitAppNormalizeFailed=4`, `ReasonBuildSubmitUnknown=5`) + `SubmitBuildStage` enum + `stageToReason` canonical mapping + Stringer round-trip + scrub helper reuse. **T1.2** exported `source.ScrubInternalTerms` (Sprint 5 T2.3 hardened version inherits F-code-1/F-sec-1/F-sec-2 fixes verbatim — no scrub duplication). **T2.2** `services.wrapSubmitBuildError(stage, err)` discriminates by STAGE LABEL emitted by the orchestrator at the wrap site (per ADR D2 — more reliable than substring matching for in-process Go orchestration errors); EC-3 preserves `ErrDeployInProgress` + typed `*LoopZeroError` (ScalingAmbiguous, ReplicasExceedPlan) via `errors.Is`/`errors.As` short-circuits at wrap entry. **T2.2 wire-in** replaces all 7 `fmt.Errorf` sites in `build_orchestrator_submit.go` (lines 58/73/99/114/144/170/194 → `wrapSubmitBuildError(StageXxx, err)`). **T2.3** new `pkg/errors.NewBuildSubmitFailedFromReason` map-driven composition (`summaryByBuildSubmitReason` mirror of Sprint 5 `summaryByPushReason`) emitting `"<actionable summary>: <verbatim detail>"` with new canonical code `BUILD_SUBMIT_FAILED` (distinct from `INTERNAL_ERROR` so loopzero-cli + dashboards + telemetry switch cleanly; CLI exit code stays 3 per Rule 4 existing unknown-code fallback). **T3.1** extended `mapBuildError` ladder with `services.AsSubmitBuildError` dispatch BEFORE the generic fallback; existing ladder order verbatim-preserved (DEPLOY_IN_PROGRESS short-circuit at lines 549-557 still wins; typed `*LoopZeroError` dispatch at lines 547-559 untouched). Test footprint (all GREEN under `go test -race`): new `TestSubmitBuildErrorReason_StringerRoundTrip` (7 cases incl. EC-6 out-of-range guard); `TestSubmitBuildError_UnwrapsThroughFmtErrorf` (Unwrap chain); `TestSubmitBuildError_ScrubsInternalTermsInDetail` (Rule 3 scrub via canonical helper); `TestWrapSubmitBuildError_NewDiscriminators` (5 positive + 2 negative subtests mirroring Sprint 5 Vercel pattern) + 3 sentinel preservation tests (EC-3 ErrDeployInProgress + typed `*LoopZeroError` + nil-input); `TestNewBuildSubmitFailedFromReason_ComposesDetail` (7 cases incl. #34-specific regression guard asserting "Something went wrong" NEVER surfaces when reason is discriminated); `TestMapBuildError_*` (4 ladder dispatch + regression-protector tests). Sprint 4 (#29/#31/#32) + Sprint 5 (#33) regression suites all green. Engineering side ready for GHCR canonical rollout (sibling of Sprint 5 commit `e9e7c32f`). Plan: `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/qa-sprint6-cleanup-2026-06-25-plan.md` SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS.

- **`scrubInternalTerms` hardened against credential-exposure regex bug + scrub coverage gaps** — review-found follow-up to the Sprint 5 commit. **F-code-1 HIGH:** the `Authorization:` redactor used `\S+` which consumed only the FIRST whitespace-separated token, so for `Authorization: Bearer ghp_<secret>` the regex replaced only `Authorization: Bearer`, leaving the credential value exposed in user-facing `Detail`. Replaced with `[^\r\n]+` to consume the full header value to end-of-line. Added defense-in-depth pattern `(Bearer|Basic)\s+[A-Za-z0-9+/=_\-.]{16,}` for inline credential leaks that appear without the header keyword (F-code-2 MEDIUM). **F-sec-1 MEDIUM:** the internal-CIDR scrub previously covered only `10.0.0.0/8`; production CNIs (Calico `192.168.0.0/16`, EKS vpc-cni `172.16.0.0/12`) would have leaked through. Combined pattern now covers all 3 RFC1918 ranges. **F-sec-2 MEDIUM:** the literal product names "Gitea" / "MinIO" were not scrubbed (only their DNS-form hostnames were) — CR-9 violation if a downstream HTTP response body contained the literal product name. Added `(?i)\bgitea\b` → `source backend` and `(?i)\bminio\b` → `object store` patterns BEFORE the DNS-form scrubs so the DNS rule still matches the normalized hostname. **F-code-3 LOW:** corrected comment in `wrapDownstreamPushError` that wrongly claimed the network-class branch composes verbatim cause in Detail (it's intentionally fixed; cause stays available via `errors.Unwrap`). Tests: 7 new subtests added to `TestPushBundleError_ScrubsInternalTerms` (Bearer/Basic/token Authorization variants + inline Bearer + 172.16/12 + 192.168/16 + public-IP negative + literal Gitea/GITEA word). All `go test -race ./api/internal/source/... ./pkg/errors/... ./api/internal/routes/...` GREEN. Review evidence: `.claude/knowledge-base/reviews/qa-sprint5-cleanup-2026-06-25-review-2026-06-25.md`.

- **loopzero-cloud#33 engine.API SOURCE_UPLOAD_FAILED catchall mis-classifies non-rejection downstream errors as "remote rejected the push. Verify your project name is unused" (misleading on fresh project names)** — Sprint 5 plan `qa-sprint5-cleanup-2026-06-25` v1.0. Root cause: Sprint 4 typed-error refactor (`loopzero-cloud#31`) replaced the legacy "Ensure your git history is clean" message, but `wrapDownstreamPushError` defaulted every non-network downstream failure to `ReasonRemoteRejectedPush`, producing equally-misleading text on FRESH project names. Fix: wrap-site discrimination (canonical `flyctl` pattern per blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/qa-sprint5-cleanup-2026-06-25-blueprint.md`) + verbatim cause preservation in user-facing message (canonical Vercel CLI pattern, `process-deployment.test.ts:17-19`). **T1.1** added 3 new typed reasons (`ReasonSourceBackendAuthFailure=6`, `ReasonSourceBackendCapacity=7`, `ReasonUnknownDownstream=8`). **T2.2** added discriminators for auth (401/403/permission-denied/Unauthorized/Forbidden), capacity (507/quota-exceeded/no-space-left/insufficient-storage), and narrowed genuine-remote-rejected to literal `pre-receive hook declined` / `pre-receive hook failed` (so the route handler's gitea-race DEPLOY_IN_PROGRESS ladder continues to intercept generic "remote rejected" first — EC-3 preserved). Default fallback NOW `ReasonUnknownDownstream` with the verbatim cause in `Detail`. **T2.3** added `scrubInternalTerms` regex scrubber invoked at `NewPushBundleError` construction time — strips cluster-internal hostnames (`gitea.*.svc.cluster.local`, `minio.*.svc.cluster.local`), DNS suffix, internal CIDR (`10.x.x.x:port`), Authorization headers and bearer tokens (Rule 3 enforcement). **T2.4** refactored `pkg/errors.NewSourceUploadFailedFromPushReason` to map-driven composition (`summaryByPushReason`) emitting `"<actionable summary>: <verbatim detail>"`. Test footprint: new `wrap_downstream_test.go` (10 positive+negative discriminator subtests + EC-3 sentinel + nil-input); extended Stringer round-trip (+3 reasons); new `TestPushBundleError_ScrubsInternalTerms` (8 cases — 6 scrub + 2 negative for legit external hostnames); new `TestNewSourceUploadFailedFromPushReason_ComposesDetail` (7 cases including #33 regression guard asserting "Verify your project name is unused" NEVER surfaces). All tests GREEN under `go test -race`. Sprint 4 regressions GREEN. Phase 3 canonical 3-script triplet: `check-dev-public-deploy-error-surface-syntax_test.sh` (hermetic 20 assertions) + `dev-public-deploy-error-surface_test.sh` (LIVE 4-scenario OPERATOR HARD STOP per cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate.md) + `dogfood-deploy-error-surface.sh` (aggregate wrapper). 3 allowlist entries added. Discovery plan + blueprint scored SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 89.0; implementation plan SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 70.0 (heuristic vague-AC cap). Pre-existing `validator_services.go:128` errorlint warning logged (commit `2068921a` 2026-06-05, unrelated). LIVE-PROOF + GitHub close on `loopzero-cloud#33` pending the Cleanroom + sponsor JUNTOS HARD STOP.

- **loopzero-cloud#31 engine.API SOURCE_UPLOAD_FAILED Message is enganoso ("Ensure your git history is clean" when git history WAS clean)** — Phase 3 of `qa-sprint4-cleanup-2026-06-25` v1.1 (T3.1 + T3.2 + T3.3 atomic). Root cause: `PushBundle` collapsed every failure mode into the canonical untyped `fmt.Errorf` chain, surfaced via `loopzeroerrors.ErrSourceUploadFailed.Message = "Failed to upload source code. Ensure your git history is clean and try again."` — actively misleading because the QA Red Team's git history WAS clean (the real failure was a backend connection refused). **T3.2** introduced typed `*source.PushBundleError` with 5 discriminated reasons (`ReasonSourceBackendUnreachable` / `ReasonRemoteRejectedPush` / `ReasonBundleMalformed` / `ReasonBundleTooLarge` / `ReasonArchiveReadError`) + canonical Stringer. **EC-6 absorption**: explicit int values (NOT iota) + lookup-table `String()` method so the wire form is the string label — future inserts won't shift persisted reason ints or break downstream pinned tests. New `MaxBundleBytes = 64 << 20` constant for the size ceiling. **T3.2 wraps the downstream untyped error** via `wrapDownstreamPushError` with heuristic discrimination of network-class errors (connection refused / no such host / dial tcp / TLS handshake / context deadline) → `ReasonSourceBackendUnreachable`. **T3.3 wires the handler ladder** at `api/internal/routes/source.go:281-330` with the EC-3-mandated precedence order: (1) `errors.Is(err, source.ErrInvalidTenantOrProject)` → 400 INVALID_TENANT_OR_PROJECT (UNCHANGED); (2) `source.IsGiteaRaceCondition(err)` substring match → 409 DEPLOY_IN_PROGRESS (UNCHANGED — F-03 2026-05-14 fix preserved verbatim, now consults the typed-error's embedded cause via `Error()`); (3) NEW `source.AsPushBundleError(err)` → 500 SOURCE_UPLOAD_FAILED with reason-specific Message via new `loopzeroerrors.NewSourceUploadFailedFromPushReason(reasonLabel)` helper at `pkg/errors/errors.go`; (4) fallback → neutral `ErrSourceUploadFailed` Message updated from misleading legacy text to canonical neutral `"The source upload failed. Please retry; if the issue persists, contact support."`. **CR-9 cloud abstraction**: all reason-specific Messages say "source backend" NOT "Gitea", "remote" NOT "git push". 7 new tests at `api/internal/source/pushbundle_error_test.go § Sprint 4 T3.1` (Stringer round-trip + IsGiteaRaceCondition canonical set + Unwrap chain + 4 per-reason PushBundle behavioral tests) + 3 EC-3 regression-assurance tests at `api/internal/routes/source_typed_error_test.go § T3.3` (INVALID_TENANT_OR_PROJECT preservation + DEPLOY_IN_PROGRESS preservation when gitea-race substring inside typed wrap + 4 typed reasons map to specific Messages WITHOUT legacy "Ensure your git history is clean" text). All Phase 3 source + routes + pkg/errors GREEN. Pre-existing baseline drift in `api/internal/argo` (Node:22.12.0 vs Node:20.11.1-alpine fixture) + `api/internal/contract` (spec-paths-registered) failures unrelated to Sprint 4 (verified by stashing changes and re-running). Plan: `loopzero/.claude/knowledge-base/plans/qa-sprint4-cleanup-2026-06-25-plan.md` v1.1 Phase 3. Closes getloopscale/loopzero-cloud#31.

- **loopzero-cloud#29 engine.API services.json validator surfaces only the FIRST violation, masking simultaneous field errors** — Phase 2 of `qa-sprint4-cleanup-2026-06-25` v1.1 (T2.1 + T2.2 + T2.3 + T2.4 atomic). Root cause: `api/internal/source/validator_services.go::ValidateServicesJSONNative` walked `leaf := ve.Causes[0]` (FIRST leaf only) when surfacing schema violations, so a payload with 3 simultaneous violations (e.g. invalid project name + bad runtime enum + bad port) returned only the project-name error — forcing the user into a cascading retry loop. **Sprint 4 T2.2** extends `*ValidationError` with `Errors []FieldError` populated by a recursive `flattenJSONSchemaCauses` walk of the FULL `Causes` tree (depth-first, left-to-right). Legacy `ve.Field`/`ve.Message` scalars remain for backward-compat (they mirror `Errors[0]`). **EC-2 absorption**: introduced `NewValidationError(field, message) *ValidationError` constructor and migrated all 6 pre-existing `&ValidationError{...}` literal sites (5 in `validator_services.go`, 1 in `validator_env.go`) so `len(Errors) >= 1` always holds for any consumer that switched from scalars to the array. **EC-4 absorption** (T2.4): extended `pkg/errors/http/writer.go::problemDetails` with an `Errors []FieldError` extension member (RFC 9457 § 3.2 — field name `errors` chosen to avoid collision with the canonical scalar `detail` field), plus new sibling helper `WriteJSONWithErrors`. **T2.3 wiring**: `SourceHandler.Upload` at `api/internal/routes/source.go:205-230` converts the aggregated `source.FieldError` slice into `errorshttp.FieldError` and surfaces ALL violations in the response body's `errors[]` array via the new `writeLoopZeroErrorWithFieldErrors` helper (`api/internal/routes/projects.go:481-501`). 2 NEW regression tests at `validator_services_test.go § Sprint 4 plan qa-sprint4-cleanup-2026-06-25 T2.1`: `TestValidateServicesJSONNative_AggregatesAllFieldErrors_closes29` asserts `len(Errors) >= 2` for a 3-violation payload; `TestValidateServicesJSONNative_EmptyPayload_PopulatesErrorsSlicePerEC2` locks the EC-2 invariant that legacy single-violation sites populate `Errors[0]`. Full source pkg + routes + pkg/errors: PASS. Plan: `loopzero/.claude/knowledge-base/plans/qa-sprint4-cleanup-2026-06-25-plan.md` v1.1 Phase 2. Closes getloopscale/loopzero-cloud#29.

- **loopzero-cloud#16 engine.API broken access control: tenant 'owner' alcanca admin POST /admin/tenants/evict sem X-Service-Auth** — Phase 1 of `qa-sprint2-cleanup-2026-06-24` v1.1 (T1.0 + T1.2 + T1.3 atomic). Root cause (EC-1 of edge-case review): `middleware.RequireAdminService` at `api/internal/middleware/auth.go:178-194` accepted BOTH `LOOPZERO_ADMIN_SERVICE_AUTH_SECRET` (canonical) AND `LOOPZERO_SERVICE_AUTH_SECRET` (legacy) via `match(canonical) || match(canonicalPrev) || match(legacy) || match(legacyPrev)`. The cloud `/engine/*` proxy adds `X-Service-Auth: $LOOPZERO_SERVICE_AUTH_SECRET` to EVERY forwarded user-JWT request, so any tenant-owner JWT routed via the proxy passed the admin gate. T1.0 narrows the middleware to canonical-only (drops `legacy` + `legacyPrev` from the match expression); the legacy env remains valid for the user-JWT proxy on NON-admin routes (consumed by `AuthWithTTL`). T1.2 migrates the evict route from inline X-Service-Auth check to chi canonical `r.With(middleware.RequireAdminService).Post(...)` wrap (`api/internal/server/router.go:143-156`) + removes the inline check from the handler body (`api/internal/routes/admin_evict.go`). New regression coverage (5 tests): `TestRequireAdminService_RejectsLegacySecret` + `TestRequireAdminService_AcceptsCanonical` + `TestRequireAdminService_AcceptsCanonicalPrevious` (middleware-level RED→GREEN); `TestAdminEvict_LegacyProxySecret_Returns403` + `TestAdminEvict_CanonicalAdminSecret_Reachable` (handler-level); `TestRouter_AllRoutesRegistered/evict_route_has_RequireAdminService_middleware` (EC-4 chi middleware-chain wiring assertion, defense against future refactor dropping the wrap). T1.3 backlog entry tracks pin + sibling admin endpoint migration (deferred per ADR D1(b) scope discipline; see `.claude/knowledge-base/backlog.md`). Sprint 1 `TestAdminEvict_EnqueueFailure_SanitizesErrorBody` preserved (no regression in #20 sanitization). Closes loopzero-cloud#16.

- **loopzero-cloud#20 engine.API /admin/tenants/evict 500 leaks k8s/ArgoCD internals (CWE-209)** — T2.1 of plan `qa-sprint1-cleanup-2026-06-24` v1.2. Replaced single `jsonError(w, 500, "ENQUEUE_FAILED", fmt.Sprintf("Failed to enqueue eviction: %v", err))` with canonical split per ADR D6: (a) `slog.Error("eviction enqueue failed", "err", err, "job_id", jobID, "tenants_matched_count", len(matched))` — DETAILED, server-side only (Loki/audit ingest); (b) `jsonError(w, 500, "ENQUEUE_FAILED", "Eviction queue temporarily unavailable. Please retry.")` — GENERIC for client. Verified via new `TestAdminEvict_EnqueueFailure_SanitizesErrorBody` (PASS): injecting an err containing `system:serviceaccount:loopzero-system:default` + `applications.argoproj.io` + `loopzero-system` + `create resource` + `API group` produces a 500 response body with ZERO leak terms while preserving the canonical `code:"ENQUEUE_FAILED"` + generic message. The detailed err is preserved in `slog` for operator debugging. Per Unbreakable Rule 8 (fail-loud-internally + generic-externally) + OWASP A09 Logging & Monitoring.

- **loopzero-cloud#14 device-flow CSRF cookie Secure attribute — LIVE-validated on dev-public 2026-06-24.** Helmfile sync applied `hydra.dev: false` overlay; Hydra Deployment rolled out with `--dev` REMOVED from container args. LIVE probe `curl -sk -i https://auth-dev.loopzero.example/oauth2/device/verify?user_code=PROBE` returns `Set-Cookie: ory_hydra_device_csrf=...; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=None` — canonical RFC 6265bis-12 §5.4.7 cookie shape. Cookie name canonical (NO `_dev` suffix), confirming dev mode is OFF. `dogfood-device --live-only` 3/3 scenarios GREEN. Evidence file: `.claude/knowledge-base/evidence/loopzero-cloud-14/2026-06-24-live-probe-set-cookie.txt`. **QA Red Team can probe dev-public end-to-end starting now.**

### Security

- **HIGH CVE GHSA-r9w3-57w2-gch2 risk-accepted on dev-public via allowlist with 90-day sunset (PROVISIONAL).** Attack vector requires admin OAuth2 token (PR:H per CVSS) which sponsor controls in dev-public. Production environments (NOT dev-public) are NOT affected — they will be on v26.2.0 from the start per the deferred upgrade slice. Red Team WILL flag this CVE in dogfood reports; operator response per ADR: "documented, accepted with sunset, tracker for v26.2.0 upgrade slice filed".
  - **UQ1 ANSWERED LIVE 2026-06-24:** The deployed dev-public Hydra image is **already `docker.io/oryd/hydra:v26.2.0`** (NOT v2.3.0 as the deps-audit inferred from the chart values comment). The HIGH CVE GHSA-r9w3-57w2-gch2 was fixed in `2.3.1-0.20260320110106-0b84568fffcc`; v26.2.0 (2026-03-20 calendar release) contains the fix via the new `secrets.pagination` config field documented in blueprint Q1. The CVE is effectively CLOSED in the deployed runtime. The allowlist remains as documented audit trail per `deps-audit-allowlist.txt` schema; the follow-up `hydra-v26-upgrade-canonical-fix-cve` slice originally planned to close the CVE via upgrade is **NOT REQUIRED** (already-closed). Operator should instead file `hydra-image-pin-doc-update` to fix the stale chart-values comment that misled deps-audit.

LIVE-validated: live-proof `--check=stripe` exits 0 with `PASS=4 SKIP=1 FAIL=0` (mandatory L1+L2+L3 PASS; advisory L2 pricing SKIP per ADR D3 — sponsor-pending Stripe dashboard product creation). Materializes Secret `loopzero-stripe-api-keys` with data key `STRIPE_API_KEY` from `~/.loopzero/dev-credentials.json.stripe.api_key`. Mode 0600 enforced + graceful SKIP on placeholder + EC-2 prefix guard (rejects publishable_key `pk_*` pasted into api_key field).

Aligns Secret key with the env var name `cloud/internal/config/config.go:158` reads via `envOr("STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET", "")`. envFrom secretRef exposes Secret data keys as env var names verbatim, so writing key `whsec` produced env var `whsec` (which Go ignores) instead of `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`. ### Fixed
- **Chaos-test gitea-outage canonical-by-construction post-decomposition: 2 LIVE-validated fixes during 2026-06-24 mandate chain** — both surfaced during Phase 5 CHAOS-TEST execution against fresh-Cleanroom dev-public state. Result: `task test:chaos:gitea-outage` exits 0 with `✅ Steady-state SLO held during experiment ✓ Hypothesis validated`.
  - `infra/tests/runner/run-chaos.sh` — Prometheus URL was hardcoded `prometheus-server.loopzero-system.svc.cluster.local` (legacy pre-decomposition path). Canonical post-decomposition state: Prometheus lives in `monitoring` namespace. Fix: default to `prometheus-server.monitoring.svc.cluster.local`. Closes silent SLO-query empty-result regression that read like a chaos hypothesis failure.
  - `infra/tests/chaos/manifests/gitea-outage.yaml` — SLO query referenced legacy metric names (`loopzero_releases_ready_over_timeout` + `loopzero_http_requests_total`) that no longer exist post-decomposition. Canonical names: `loopzero_releases_pending` (release reaper queue-depth gauge) + `loopzero_api_requests_total` (per-API HTTP counter). Also reshape query to use `count(series) > 0` instead of `max_over_time(...)[5m] > 0` — the latter requires 5min of continuous samples which a 120s chaos partition window can break, falsely triggering hypothesis-failed. Counts always reflect Prometheus's current view of active series.

- **Post-Cleanroom canonical seeds: 4 LIVE-validated fixes during 2026-06-23 chain execution** — all surfaced during the CLEANROOM FULL + UP + seeds + DOGFOOD FULL + LIVE-PROOF run on dev-public. Each fix preserves the canonical chain so future Cleanrooms reach 13/13 dogfood PASS without manual intervention:
  - `infra/helm/chaos-mesh/values.yaml` — orphan-cleanup CronJob image changed from `bitnami/kubectl:1.32` (removed from Docker Hub Aug 2025 per Bitnami deprecation) to `alpine/k8s:1.32.0` (canonical Alpine bundle: kubectl + helm + bash). Closes ImagePullBackOff regression in dev-public + standalone-multi chaos-mesh installs.
  - `infra/scripts/local/seed-argocd-token-canonical.sh` — argocd-server runs `--insecure` mode in k3d (svc port 80→8080 plaintext; svc port 443→8080 has NO TLS termination → HTTPS handshake fails). Port-forward switched from `14444:443` → `14444:80`, REST calls switched from `https://localhost:14444` → `http://localhost:14444` (3 endpoints: /api/v1/version, /api/v1/session, /api/v1/account/loopzero-api/token). Added 30s readiness poll loop (`for i in $(seq 1 30)`) because `--retry-connrefused` alone is insufficient — kubectl `Forwarding from...` message appears BEFORE the pod->localhost path is reliably routable; explicit poll matches manual probe pattern reaching 200 OK at ~6s steady-state.
  - `infra/scripts/local/seed-vault-cross-cluster-addr-canonical.sh` — openbao Service exposes `https` port (NodePort 30200) per cert-manager TLS in vault cluster, but the script was probing `name=="http"` (lookup empty) AND emitting `http://${IP}:${PORT}` scheme. loopzero-api would log `vault: AppRole login returned 400: Client sent an HTTP request to an HTTPS server`. Fixed jsonpath to `?(@.name=="https")` + scheme to `https://`. Closes vault AppRole auth chain in dev-public cross-cluster topology.
  - `infra/scripts/local/seed-tenant-fixture-canonical.sh` — Tenant CR YAML used wrong apiVersion (`loopzero.loopzero.example/v1` instead of canonical `loopzero.io/v1` per `operators/api/v1/tenant_types.go` PackageGroup) AND was missing required spec fields per CRD admission validation (`spec.gitops`, `spec.limits`, `spec.preview`). dogfood-environments Scenario E was failing with `INTERNAL_ERROR: k8s: get tenant CR for AddEnvironment: tenants.loopzero.io not found`. Extended seed to apply full canonical spec matching tn-e2etest reference (cellID + displayName + plan + environments.production + gitops branch/repoName + limits cpu/memory/storage/build_concurrency + preview maxEnvironments/ttlHours).

  `api/cmd/setup_server.go` via `StatusHandler.SetPlanTierStore(limitsStore)`
  alongside the existing `ScaleHandler.SetPlanTierStore` call. Optional
  field (omitempty) — clients fall back to `'starter'` when older engines
  omit it. Closes `#123` of `qa-dogfood-cleanup-2026-06-21` canonically
  (replaces the stopgap that depended on Known Gap #116 cloud BFF billing
  read-side, which is 404).
- `infra/tests/charts/generate_secrets_context_pin_test.go` — hermetic Go regression-lock for
  the `infra/helmfile/scripts/generate-secrets.sh` `--context` pin (closes #115). Pre-fix the
  script did not pin `kubectl --context`, so any active context residue (e.g. `k3d-loopzero-vault`
  from a prior T0.1 / vault-bootstrap session) silently misprovisioned Secrets to the wrong
  cluster, and `task setup` failed at `infra:install:mgmt` with the secrets landed in
  loopzero-vault. Asserts (a) the canonical `KUBECTL_CONTEXT="${KUBECTL_CONTEXT:-k3d-loopzero-mgmt}"`
  default is declared + (b) the `kubectl()` function override injects
  `--context="$KUBECTL_CONTEXT"` on every call + (c) audit-trail cites issue #115. Negative-
  control mutates each piece independently and asserts the matching check fires. Pure
  hermetic — NO kubectl, NO network.
- `infra/tests/charts/openbao_cert_san_canonical_test.go` — hermetic render-level regression-
  lock for the OpenBao Certificate SAN canonical contract (closes post-/review MEDIUM
  "cert-SAN dependency only LIVE-spike proven, no hermetic guard"). Renders
  `infra/helm/openbao-cert` via `helm template`, extracts the `kind: Certificate name: openbao`
  block, asserts `host.k3d.internal` IS present in `dnsNames`. The slice's D1 (x509-clean
  base) depends on this SAN coverage — without it, the mgmt-pod → host.k3d.internal:8200
  reach fails TLS verification (F1-class blanket failure mode). Negative-control mutates the
  rendered output to drop the SAN entry and asserts the guard fires.

  `-c loopzero-api`) formalized in `knowledge-base/plans/k3d-host-published-vault-reach-plan.md`

- `infra/tests/charts/charts_go_tests_wired_test.go` — meta-guard locking the

  `check-openbao-transit-quality-gates-wired_test.sh` (J-22 iter 27), but homed as Go (not

  detectable.
- `infra/tests/charts/dogfood_all_repo_root_test.go` — hermetic Go regression-lock for the

- `infra/tests/charts/live_proof_env_file_test.go` — hermetic Go regression-lock for the

- `infra/tests/charts/connect_canonical_fixture_container_name_test.go` — hermetic Go regression-lock

  elsewhere in the project).

  theatre. Pure hermetic — NO kubectl, NO network, NO cluster. Follows the sister-pattern of

### Notes
- The manual SQL `UPDATE releases SET status='ACTIVE'` plus ~10 LIVE cluster mutations (chaos-mesh helm install, cosign-signing-key Secret mirror mgmt→build, Gitea `loopzero` org create, ArgoCD admin token mint + sync, cluster-issuer chart install, Tenant CR seed, ArgoCD AppProject create, OPENBAO_VAULT_ADDR cross-cluster wiring, loopzero-stripe-webhook Secret materialization, Stripe whsec dev placeholder in `~/.loopzero/dev-credentials.json`) applied during session Cleanroom 2026-06-22→23 are explicitly NOT part of this commit per ADR D3 carve-out — canonical solutions are tracked in M2 step 1-11 of the roadmap upstream.

- `qa-dogfood-cleanup-2026-06-21` — backend slice closing 5 of 11 QA-surfaced

  + CLI pieces ship in their own repos):
    - (#118) `domain.ProjectSummary.LastDeployAt` switched from value-type

      operator-shell `LOOPZERO_PLAN_TIER=starter` residue can no longer shadow
      the canonical dev-public tier (ADR-Q7). Hermetic guard at
      `infra/tests/charts/dev_public_team_tier_seed_test.go`.
    - (#124) `pkg/domain.PermanentEnvs = []string{"production", "preview"}`
      canonicalized as the SoT enum for permanent envs per project
      (ADR-Q5). `api/internal/routes/projects.go` POST handler now seeds
      BOTH at project.created in the canonical order (production fatal
      on failure / preview tolerant per Vercel-style derivability).
      3 new hermetic tests at `pkg/domain/constants_permanent_envs_test.go`.
    - (#126 Phase A) `api/internal/audit/domain_events.go` ships the
      closed-enum + 2-phase rollout scaffold (feature flag
      `AUDIT_ENFORCE_DOMAIN_ENUM`, default OFF). Phase A WARN-only path
      via `ValidateAction()` + `UnknownActionWarning()`; Phase B (reject
      mode) flips after at least one release of WARN observation. 5

      secret/build/release/environment/domain/api_key domains.
    - (#127 backend echo) `pkg/domain.DefaultPreviewEnv = "preview"` so
      the canonical PaaS lexicon (preview + production) is enforced
      cross-language alongside the dashboard env-terminology audit.
- `infra/helmfile/scripts/generate-secrets.sh` — pin `kubectl --context` to
  `KUBECTL_CONTEXT="${KUBECTL_CONTEXT:-k3d-loopzero-mgmt}"` via a `kubectl()` shell-function
  override that injects `--context="$KUBECTL_CONTEXT"` on every call (closes #115). All bare
  `kubectl` invocations downstream automatically inherit the canonical mgmt context by
  construction. Without the pin, an active `k3d-loopzero-vault` context (residue from a prior
  T0.1 / vault-bootstrap session) silently misprovisioned Secrets to the wrong cluster, and
  `task setup` failed at `infra:install:mgmt` with the secrets landed in loopzero-vault. Override
  via `KUBECTL_CONTEXT` env var for non-k3d targets (DOKS prod, drill cluster). Regression-
  locked by `infra/tests/charts/generate_secrets_context_pin_test.go`.
- `taskfiles/quality.yml` — wire `infra/tests/charts/` Go guards into `task quality:gates`
  (closes /review HIGH 2026-06-20). Pre-fix the slice's regression protection (16 hermetic Go
  guards) existed but never ran in pre-push; only `cd infra/tests && go test ./unit/...` was
  invoked. Adds the canonical invocation `cd infra/tests && go test ./charts/ -count=1` with a
  fail-loud helmfile pre-flight: the gate aborts with operator-actionable diagnostics if
  `helmfile` is absent from PATH OR `LOOPZERO_TEST_SKIP_HELMFILE=1` is set, preventing the silent
  SKIP of `TestVaultHostPublishedReach` (the central helmfile-render guard) — closes the
  related MEDIUM. Verified GREEN on the droplet 2026-06-20 (helmfile present): all 16 chart
  guards run + the new `TestChartsGoTestsWired` meta-guard auto-runs inside `./charts/`. No
  fallout from enabling `./charts/` — baseline was already 39 PASS / 0 FAIL / 0 SKIP.
- `archive/canonical-tests-v1/scripts/local/dogfood-all.sh` — REPO_ROOT canonical 4-`..` climbs
  (NOT 3) for the post-archive script relocation (issue #106). The script lives 4 levels deep
  at `loopzero/archive/canonical-tests-v1/scripts/local/`; the pre-fix 3-climb landed at
  `loopzero/archive/` instead of `loopzero/`, so WRAPPERS paths
  `archive/canonical-tests-v1/scripts/local/dogfood-*.sh` resolved to `loopzero/archive/archive/...`
  and every wrapper hit `[[ ! -f "$wrapper" ]]` → 13/13 SKIP "script missing on disk" with a
  paradoxical `verdict=PASS` despite zero wrappers run. Post-fix `dogfood-all.sh --live-only`
  resolves wrappers and invokes them (per-wrapper PASS/FAIL surfaces real journey state, no
  longer harness SKIP-missing). Regression-locked by
  `infra/tests/charts/dogfood_all_repo_root_test.go`.
- `archive/canonical-tests-v1/scripts/local/live-proof-dev-public.sh` — migrate from dead
  `ENV_FILE=/home/paulo/loopzero/.env` (pre-M2 absolute path; file deleted in decomposition) to
  canonical `CREDS_FILE=$HOME/.loopzero/dev-credentials.json` JSON SoT (issue #107). Pre-fix the
  script exited 2 silently after one line because `env_get`'s `grep` against the missing file
  under `set -euo pipefail` aborted execution. The new `env_get()` maps each `LOOPZERO_*` logical
  key to its canonical `~/.loopzero/dev-credentials.json` path via `jq`. Keys absent from the JSON
  schema (e.g., `LOOPZERO_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`) return empty so the caller's Layer-1 check
  bad()s honestly — surfacing the schema gap, not papering over it. Sibling canonical scripts
  use the same SoT: `infra/scripts/local/secrets-seed-dev.sh:26`,
  `infra/scripts/local/setup-dev-public-dns.sh:36`. Regression-locked by
  `infra/tests/charts/live_proof_env_file_test.go`.
- `infra/scripts/local/connect-canonical-fixture.sh` — workspace-relative discovery of the
  canonical LoopZero CLI binary `loopzero-cli/bin/loopzero` when neither `$LOOPZERO_BIN` is exported nor `loopzero`
  is on `PATH` (issue #108). Post-M2 decomposition the LoopZero CLI lives in a sibling repo; the
  master orchestrator `seed-post-cleanroom-all.sh` invokes Step 11 without pre-exporting
  `LOOPZERO_BIN`, and pre-fix this emitted `WARN+return-2` because the script defaulted to bare
  `loopzero` on `PATH`. The new 1-line derivation chains: (a) operator-exported `LOOPZERO_BIN`, (b)
  `command -v loopzero`, (c) workspace-relative `loopzero-cli/bin/loopzero` via `readlink -f`, (d) literal
  `loopzero` fallback so the existing WARN path remains the last-resort signal. LIVE-verified:
  Step 11 runs to `DONE — fixture connected` with no operator-exported LOOPZERO_BIN. Regression-
  locked by `infra/tests/charts/loopzero_bin_workspace_discovery_test.go`.
- `infra/scripts/local/connect-canonical-fixture.sh` — canonical container name `loopzero-api` (NOT
  `api`) for `kubectl exec deployment/loopzero-api -c <container> --` inside `mint_app_jwt()`. The
  chart name + Service name + container name are all `loopzero-api` per
  `infra/helm/loopzero-api/templates/deployment.yaml`. The previous `-c api` selected zero matching
  containers; kubectl errored to stderr, the script's `2>/dev/null` suppressed the message + the
  `|| true` swallowed the non-zero exit, so `$app_id` ended up empty and the script reported
  `cannot resolve GITHUB_APP_ID` while the env var IS present in the pod. Caught during the
  2026-06-20 Cleanroom-fresh `seed-post-cleanroom-all.sh --verify --json` run that returned
  `pass=16 fail=1` with Step 11 as the only FAIL (massive improvement from prior `pass=11 fail=6`
  proving the 4 root-cause fixes — reach + hijack + bao_auth + SAN — held Cleanroom-survivable).
  Post-fix re-run returned `pass=17 fail=0 verdict=PASS`. Regression-locked by hermetic Go guard
  `infra/tests/charts/connect_canonical_fixture_container_name_test.go` with positive + negative
  control.
- `infra/scripts/local/vault-bootstrap-dev.sh` — rewrite host-scoped kubeconfig server URL to a
  SAN-aligned name reachable from the OpenBao pod's network namespace BEFORE writing
  `auth/k8s-mgmt/config`. The kubeconfig on the operator droplet resolves the mgmt API as
  `https://0.0.0.0:6443` (k3d's host-published bind address). Two requirements must hold
  simultaneously: (a) reachability — from the vault pod (loopzero-vault cluster, Jeito A), the URL
  must resolve and connect to the mgmt API server on the host; (b) TLS hostname/SAN — OpenBao's
  Go HTTP client verifies the server cert strictly. The k3d-mgmt API cert SAN list is
  `DNS: k3d-loopzero-mgmt-server-0, k3d-loopzero-mgmt-serverlb, kubernetes(.default(.svc(.cluster.local))),
  localhost` + the in-cluster IPs. `host.k3d.internal` is NOT in the SAN list — using it triggers
  SAN-mismatch which OpenBao surfaces as a generic blanket "permission denied" from
  `auth/k8s-mgmt/login` for every role. The canonical SAN-aligned + cross-cluster-reachable name
  is the k3d serverlb container `k3d-${cluster_name}-serverlb` (derived from `TARGET_CONTEXT`,
  e.g., `k3d-loopzero-mgmt` → `k3d-loopzero-mgmt-serverlb`). k3d injects this name into every cluster's
  CoreDNS NodeHosts via the shared docker network, so it resolves canonically from loopzero-vault.
  Surfaced + isolated during the T3.1 LIVE iteration 2026-06-20 via a 4-probe sequence
  (Probe 1 refuted H2 CA-mismatch; Probe 2 with TLS bypass returned `authenticated=true`;
  Probe 3 detected the SAN-mismatch; Probe 4 verified strict TLS via the serverlb name → HTTP
  201 + TokenReview authenticated). Warm validation: `ClusterSecretStore loopzero-vault-secrets`
  transitioned from 51min `InvalidProviderConfig` to `Ready=True reason=Valid`. Canonical close
  remains gated on the next Cleanroom-fresh `seed-post-cleanroom-all.sh --verify` 0 FAIL run
  per the cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate.
- `infra/scripts/local/seed-vault-role-id-sync.sh` — separate `VAULT_CONTEXT` (default
  `k3d-loopzero-vault`) from `MGMT_CONTEXT` (default `k3d-loopzero-mgmt`) per Jeito A INQUEBRÁVEL.
  Previous code defaulted to `k3d-loopzero-mgmt` for the openbao-0 pod lookup and emitted a fail message
  recommending `task k3d:vault:bootstrap-in-mgmt` (which is PROHIBITED — separate cluster topology
  violation). The script now correctly reads the canonical role_id from the loopzero-vault cluster +
  patches loopzero-api Deployment env in mgmt cluster.

### Changed
- `.claude/rules/cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate.md` § Sub-step 2.5 — re-scoped per ADR
  [`2026-06-20-dev-public-vault-automated-init`](.claude/knowledge-base/decisions/2026-06-20-dev-public-vault-automated-init.md)
  (sponsor-accepted risk). The witness + distributed-shard ceremony (1Password / YubiKey / LUKS USB /
  safe-deposit-box) is now scoped to **persistent / production** vaults (sole-custodian topology). The
  dev-public Cleanroom TEST tier uses an **automated** Shamir init via `vault-bootstrap-dev.sh`
  (ephemeral local shards at `~/.loopzero/cleanroom-unseal.json` 0600, no sponsor JUNTOS, ~5min wall-clock).
  Rationale: in dev-public the `~/.loopzero/dev-credentials.json` plaintext already co-locates on the
  droplet with the shards → distributed custody adds only marginal protection when the vault is NOT
  sole-custodian. **Jeito A INQUEBRÁVEL preserved**: `task k3d:vault:bootstrap-in-mgmt` remains
  PROHIBITED in any tier (separate cluster topology violation, independent of witness scope).
  Anti-patterns table re-tiered (prod/persistent vs dev-public vs both); cross-references updated.

### Added
- Hermetic regression gate `TestDevPublicVaultBootstrapDefault` in
  `infra/tests/charts/dev_public_vault_bootstrap_default_test.go`
  (T-A3 of follow-up to ADR `2026-06-20-dev-public-vault-automated-init`). Locks 4 assertions:
  (a) `seed-post-cleanroom-all.sh` invokes `vault-bootstrap-dev.sh`; (b) for
  `LOOPZERO_HELMFILE_ENV=dev-public`, the script auto-provisions loopzero-vault via `task k3d:vault:create`
  + `task k3d:vault:bootstrap` BEFORE `vault-bootstrap-dev.sh` runs (so the dev-public chain is
  solo/CI provisionable per the ADR); (c) the script NEVER invokes `task k3d:vault:bootstrap-in-mgmt`
  (Jeito A INQUEBRÁVEL — comment-line mentions of the forbidden shortcut are allowed; actual
  invocations are forbidden); (d) `cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate.md` cites the ADR so the
  witness re-scoping is auditable from the rule. Pure-text grep on canonical files; no kubectl, no
  docker, no helmfile.

### Changed
- `infra/scripts/local/vault-bootstrap-dev.sh` — Step 5.5 R3 TokenReview path FIXED. Two coupled bugs:
  (1) the WARN text on timeout said "60s" but the actual loop budget was 180s (stale text from a
  prior bump per memory `j22-aprendizado-openbao-multi-secret-chain`); (2) the `bao_auth` shell
  function used by the wait-loop was defined LATER in the script (alongside Step 6/7), so during
  Step 5.5 the call returned no stdout, the Python check evaluated `'k8s-mgmt/' in {}` → False
  every iteration, the 180s wait loop ALWAYS timed out, and `auth/k8s-mgmt/config` was NEVER
  written even when the mount was actually present. Downstream consequence: CSS loopzero-vault-secrets
  stayed `Ready=False reason=InvalidProviderConfig` (seed-post-cleanroom Step 1b FAIL), ESO
  cross-cluster workload-identity path silently broke. Fix: define `bao_auth` immediately after
  ROOT_TOKEN is set (Step 4), remove the legacy duplicate definition at Step 6/7 (replaced with
  audit-trail comment), correct the WARN text to "180s". LIVE-validated: `bao auth list` from a
  root-token shell shows `k8s-mgmt/`, `k8s-build/`, `k8s-cell-*/`, `approle/` all enabled. The
  16-assertion regression guard `check-vault-bootstrap-unseal-loop_test.sh` still passes after the
  edits.

- `infra/scripts/local/vault-bootstrap-dev.sh` — NEW Step 1.1 `kubectl wait pod openbao-0 --for=condition=Ready --timeout=300s` BEFORE Step 2 (`bao operator init`).
  LIVE finding 2026-06-20 T-A4 follow-up to ADR `2026-06-20-dev-public-vault-automated-init`: when the chain
  is invoked back-to-back with `task k3d:vault:bootstrap` (now standard via T-A2 Step 0b), the openbao
  chart returns from `helm install` before the openbao-0 pod is scheduled (`wait: false` per
  `feedback_helmfile_wait_false_for_bootstrap_deps`). The very first `kubectl exec` then fails with
  `pod openbao-0 does not have a host assigned`; `bao operator init` silently no-ops; the unseal file
  lands size-1 (just a newline); Steps 2-10 cascade-fail with "Vault is sealed". The wait gates Step 2
  on the pod actually being Ready (sealed-state-tolerant readiness probe), eliminating the race. LIVE
  validated: Step 1 PASS canonical, `loopzero-openbao-approle` Secret materialized end-to-end.

- `infra/scripts/local/seed-post-cleanroom-all.sh` — NEW Step 0 (T-A2 of follow-up to ADR
  `2026-06-20-dev-public-vault-automated-init`). When `LOOPZERO_HELMFILE_ENV=dev-public`, Step 0
  auto-provisions the SEPARATE `loopzero-vault` k3d cluster + OpenBao chart via `task k3d:vault:create`
  + `task k3d:vault:bootstrap` BEFORE Step 1 (`vault-bootstrap-dev.sh`) attempts init/unseal. Both
  tasks are idempotent: no-op on warm re-run; full provision on Cleanroom-fresh. In other envs
  (standalone-multi / unset / prod) Step 0 is skipped. **Jeito A INQUEBRÁVEL preserved**:
  `task k3d:vault:bootstrap-in-mgmt` remains PROHIBITED in every tier. Closes the dev-public chain
  to be solo/CI provisionable end-to-end per the ADR.

- Hermetic regression gate `TestHostK3dInternalCanonical` in `infra/tests/charts/host_k3d_internal_canonical_test.go`
  (T1.2 of plan `k3d-host-published-vault-reach`, ADR D6, sponsor 2026-06-20). Locks the canonical
  contract on `infra/scripts/local/setup-cross-cluster-networking.sh`: mgmt CoreDNS
  `host.k3d.internal` MUST resolve to the `k3d-loopzero` bridge **gateway**, NEVER to the
  `k3d-loopzero-workload-serverlb` container IP. 4 assertions: (a) no `docker inspect k3d-loopzero-workload-serverlb`
  for the resolution target + no `$WORKLOAD_LB_IP host.k3d.internal` NodeHosts line; (b) derivation goes
  through `docker network inspect <k3d-loopzero> -f '{{(index .IPAM.Config 0).Gateway}}'` honoring the
  `.Gateway` + `.IPAM.Config` keys; (c) the NodeHosts line written is `$GATEWAY_IP host.k3d.internal`
  (gateway-derived variable); (d) script comments cite the #111-#115 history / ADR D6 / plan slug so
  operators reading in 6 months see why the gateway choice matters. Closes the host-side name
  resolution hijack that broke the chain T1.1 proved (probe-measured 2026-06-20: bridge gateway
  reaches vault:8200 + every mgmt/workload/k8s-API host-publish port; workload-serverlb returns TCP
  RST in ~0.4ms on every platform port).

### Changed
- `infra/scripts/local/setup-cross-cluster-networking.sh` — Step 2 now derives the k3d-loopzero bridge
  gateway via `docker network inspect ${SHARED_NETWORK} -f '{{(index .IPAM.Config 0).Gateway}}'`
  instead of `docker inspect k3d-loopzero-workload-serverlb`. Step 3 writes the gateway IP as the
  `host.k3d.internal` NodeHosts target. Comments rewritten to cite plan
  `k3d-host-published-vault-reach` ADR D6 + the #111-#115 history + the 2026-06-20 probe evidence.
  Idempotent + Cleanroom-survivable: every `task setup` re-asserts the canonical resolution. LIVE
  validation on droplet 2026-06-20: mgmt-pod `curl https://host.k3d.internal:8200/v1/sys/health?uninitcode=200`
  returns HTTP 200 + the OpenBao sealed JSON via canonical CoreDNS resolution (no `--resolve` override).

- Hermetic regression gate `TestVaultHostPublishedReach` in `infra/tests/charts/vault_host_published_reach_test.go`
  (T1.1 of plan `k3d-host-published-vault-reach`, sponsor 2026-06-20). Locks the canonical
  host-publish topology for loopzero-vault → openbao cross-cluster reach onto the mechanism the T0.1
  LIVE SPIKE proved end-to-end (mgmt-pod → docker bridge gateway 172.18.0.1:8200 → k3d serverlb
  nginx `stream{}` L4 passthrough → server-0:30200 NodePort → openbao-0 pod :8200 → HTTP 200,
  TLS bytes intact, no decode_error 562). 7 assertions cover (a) Service `type: NodePort` not
  LoadBalancer, (b) `nodePort: 30200` FIXED (NOT auto-assigned — the #112 trap), (c) port/targetPort
  aligned at 8200/8200, (d) `vault-cluster.yaml` maps `8200:30200@loadbalancer` (NOT `@server:0`),
  (e) `--disable=servicelb` present (Klipper off, serverlb is sole publish path per D3),
  (f) CSS server unchanged at `host.k3d.internal:8200` (SAN-clean per D4), and (g) F-1 absorption:
  openbao chart `server.networkPolicy.enabled: false` in vault-local block + render-side guard
  asserting NO `kind: NetworkPolicy` for the openbao release. F-1 surfaced LIVE during T0.1 SPIKE
  (the chart-default policy only allows ingress from `namespaceSelector: {}` — intra-cluster pod
  sources — and DROPS NodePort traffic arriving with source IP from the host network namespace
  outside any K8s namespace). Closes issues #111, #112, #113, #114.

### Changed
- `infra/k3d/vault-cluster.yaml` — port mapping flipped from `8200:8200@server:0` (the #112 fix
  superseded by #113→#114 diagnostic chain) to `8200:30200@loadbalancer` (plan
  `k3d-host-published-vault-reach` D1, sponsor 2026-06-20). `--disable=servicelb` added (D3 —
  Klipper off, the k3d serverlb `stream{}` is the sole publish path). Header comments rewritten
  to document the proven mechanism + reference the SAN-clean property.
- `infra/helmfile/vault.helmfile.yaml.gotmpl` `vault-local` block — OpenBao Service
  `type: LoadBalancer` (the R5 of `dev-public-cross-cluster-secrets`, now superseded) flipped to
  `type: NodePort` + `nodePort: 30200` FIXED (D4/D5 — deterministic across Cleanrooms).
  `server.networkPolicy.enabled: true` flipped to `false` (F-1 absorption per T0.1 SPIKE LIVE
  confirmation). PROD block at line 289 retains `networkPolicy.enabled: true` — prod ESO reaches
  OpenBao via in-cluster Service, never via host-publish, so the chart policy is fine there.

- Hermetic regression gate `TestRuntimeEnvCompleteness` in `infra/tests/charts/env_completeness_test.go`
  (T1.1 of plan `dev-public-env-config-completeness`). Locks the dev-public runtime env/secret surface:
  reads `infra/tests/fixtures/runtime-env-manifest.txt` (200 distinct runtime rows derived from the

  runtime vars), and `env-completeness-known-gaps.txt` (explicit allowlist refusing silent debt);
  cross-references against the install-config-contract surface (CONFIG.md + SECRETS.md across 7
  charts). 5 sub-tests: anti-vacuity floor (EC-2, ≥150), producer floor (EC-5 companion, ≥30),
  completeness (0 NEW req=Y orphan + 0 NEW phantom), SECRET materialization (EC-1/D4 — every SECRET
  runtime VAR has a producer), and camelCase ↔ UPPER_SNAKE normalization (EC-4 — viteApiUrl
  ↔ VITE_API_URL). The allowlist file refuses empty justifications at parse time. Companion
  generator `_generate_runtime_manifest.py` (one-shot, NOT runtime — D2 keeps the Go guard hermetic).
- New § Sub-step 2.5 in `.claude/rules/cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate.md` documenting the
  Sponsor JUNTOS OpenBao Shamir init gate for dev-public env (issue #101). The mandate now
  explicitly slots the ceremony BETWEEN step 2 (UP) and step 3 (seeds), with the canonical
  command sequence (`task k3d:vault:create` + `task k3d:vault:bootstrap` + sponsor
  `bao operator init -key-shares=5 -key-threshold=3` + 3-of-5 unseal + provisioning
  helmfile sync), the runbook cross-link, the standalone-multi escape hatch via
  `task k3d:vault:bootstrap-in-mgmt`, and the anti-patterns table. `docs/operations/openbao-init-runbook.md`
  back-links the mandate. Hermetic regression lock
  `TestCleanroomMandateOpenBaoSponsorJUNTOSGateDocumented` asserts both files contain the
  required cross-references.
- Hermetic regression gate `TestHelmfileOverlayConfigKeysHonorSchema` in `infra/tests/charts/`
  (Cleanroom catch 2026-06-19). Scans every `infra/helmfile/values/<env>/<chart>.yaml.gotmpl`,
  parses `/config:` keys, and asserts each one is declared in the matching chart's
  `values.schema.json /properties/config/properties` whenever the schema has
  `additionalProperties:false` at that path (Slice 4 hardening). Closes the helmfile
  env-overlay drift surface that the Slice 4 EC-3 Go-deps transitive guard did not cover.
- Hermetic regression gate `TestOpenbaoInitAuthRoleSupportsBoundAudiences`
  in `infra/tests/charts/openbao_init_bound_audiences_test.go` locking
  R2 of plan `dev-public-cross-cluster-secrets` (issue #111, sponsor
  approved 2026-06-19). Verifies the `openbao-init` chart's
  `write_auth_role` helper accepts a 7th positional arg `bound_audiences`
  and emits the `bao write ... bound_audiences=<csv>` line ONLY when
  the role declares `boundAudiences` in values (back-compat preserved
  for roles that omit the field). EC-5 defence-in-depth: TokenRequest
  aud claim must match OpenBao role `bound_audiences` — closes the
  "any-audience login" gap.
- Hermetic regression gate `TestVaultBootstrapDev_TransitAutoUnseal` in
  `infra/tests/scripts/vault_bootstrap_auto_unseal_test.go` locking T2.1
  (hermetic side) of plan `dev-public-cross-cluster-secrets` (issue
  #111). Verifies the opt-in Transit auto-unseal contract: vault-bootstrap-dev.sh
  declares `AUTO_UNSEAL_MODE` (default `shamir` — backwards compat), the
  Shamir init path is guarded so it does NOT run when `AUTO_UNSEAL_MODE=transit`,
  and an EC-3 Transit-ready wait (`wait_transit_ready` probing

  `.Values.transitAutoUnsealEnabled` (default off). EC-7 (ephemeral

  file written) requires sponsor JUNTOS Cleanroom per
  `cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate § Sub-step 2.5` — not in scope here.
- Hermetic regression gate `TestCrossClusterSecretStoreContract` in
  `infra/tests/charts/cross_cluster_secrets_test.go` locking T1.2 of plan
  `dev-public-cross-cluster-secrets` (issue #111). Verifies the dev-public
  ESO `loopzero-external-secrets` overlay declares workload identity
  (`auth.kind: kubernetes` + `serviceAccountRef` + `audiences`) replacing
  the legacy AppRole shape. Asserts EC-5 contract (audiences present) and
  EC-2 contract (`server` retains canonical intra-cluster DNS so the cert

  dev-public overlay carries the cross-cluster traceability comment so a

- Hermetic regression gate `TestVaultBootstrapDev_SplitContexts` in
  `infra/tests/scripts/vault_bootstrap_split_contexts_test.go` locking T1.1 of plan

  init/unseal/approle/SPKI read), `TARGET_CONTEXT` defaults to `k3d-loopzero-mgmt`
  (mgmt-cluster side — `loopzero-openbao-approle` + `openbao-spki-pin` Secret apply where
  loopzero-api Deployment envFrom reads from); EC-4 ns-ensure precedes the Secret apply;
  EC-6 `initialized` state guard preserved against accidental destructive re-init.
  9 sub-assertions cover defaults, ns-ensure, Secret-apply context, bao-exec context,
