### Security (2026-06-10 J-22 Layer 2 code-review fixes — engineering-side closure)

Closes the 74 findings of `/loop-code-review` against commits 572911de + a5fe7797 + 07e886df via plan `2026-06-10-j22-layer2-code-review-fixes` v1.1 (SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 89.0 per `/plan-confidence`; PASS_WITH_CAVEATS per `/deps-audit`). 9 commits land 1 consensus-CRITICAL + 5 HIGH + 5 MEDIUM + 6 pre-existing RED + 7 EC absorptions. Bisect anchors: pre-fix `docs/operations/baselines/2026-06-10-j22-layer2-pre-fix.md` (07e886df), post-fix `docs/operations/baselines/2026-06-10-j22-layer2-post-fix.md` (eefa9fb2). Phase 6 LIVE sponsor walkthrough remains operator HARD STOP per `cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate`.

- **`api/cmd/setup_secret_fetch.go` brokerBearerMiddleware — X-Cell-ID server-side derivation (closes consensus-CRITICAL #13)** — the prior code path read `r.Header.Get("X-Cell-ID")` and defaulted to magic literal `"cell-default"`, so any holder of `LOOPZERO_BROKER_TOKEN` could spoof any cell and read every tenant's secrets via the synthetic `CrossClusterTokenReviewResult` carried into the EC-07 cell↔tenant binding check at `routes/internal_secret_fetch.go:143`. Fix: the header is intentionally ignored (`_ = r.Header.Get("X-Cell-ID")`) and the cellID is derived server-side from a new package-canonical constant `placement.DefaultCellID` referenced via a named local `brokerSingleClusterCellID` (closes EC-3 single source of truth — the magic literal `"cell-default"` now lives in `api/internal/placement/cells.go` only). Production multi-cluster path will replace this with `CrossClusterTokenReviewer.Review(cellID, token)` in a separate follow-up plan.
- **`api/internal/routes/internal_secret_fetch.go` URL contract redesign — composite-key scope (closes HIGH #15)** — the prior `InternalSecretReader.ReadSecret(ctx, tenantID, secretName)` SQL scoped only by `(tenant_id, name)`, silently leaking production-env secret values into preview-env Secret materializations when the same `name` existed across envs. T2.1 + ADR D2 redesigns the URL to 4 segments (`/internal/v1/secret-fetch/{tenant_id}/{secret_name}/{project}/{environment}`) so the SQL scopes by the canonical composite unique key `(tenant_id, project, environment, name)`. `SecretReadAuditEvent` gains `Project` + `Environment` fields so forensic queries against audit_log can answer "which env did the attempted cross_tenant_attempt target?".
- **`api/internal/routes/internal_secret_fetch_audit_adapter.go` audit telemetry (closes HIGH #16)** — `_ = AuditWriter.WriteAudit(...)` previously discarded the error on the most security-critical event (cross_tenant_attempt). T2.3 lands telemetry inside the adapter: on LogAudit failure, the new `loopzero_secret_audit_write_failed_total{event_type=...}` Prometheus counter is bumped + `slog.Error` records structured context (tenant_id, secret_name, project, environment, actor). Callers may keep best-effort posture (`_ = WriteAudit(...)`); failures are now observable centrally.
- **`infra/helm/loopzero-external-secrets/templates/clustersecretstore-loopzero-broker.yaml` helm fail-CLOSED guard (closes HIGH #23 + HIGH #27)** — render-time guard refuses to render when `broker.enabled=true` + `apiURL` starts with `http://` AND `envName` is not the explicit `dev-public` allowlist (EC-6 absorption: empty/unknown envName ALSO fails — no fail-open default). Same template extends the webhook URL to 4 ESO substitutions (`/internal/v1/secret-fetch/<KEY>/<PROPERTY>/<PROJECT>/<ENVIRONMENT>`) via the canonical printf double-curly escape pattern, replacing the 2-segment legacy contract.
- **`api/cmd/setup_secret_fetch_test.go` — 21 named unit tests for setup_secret_fetch.go (closes HIGH #113)** — the 171 LOC NEW security-sensitive file shipped 2026-06-10 with zero unit tests. T3.1 adds 21 named tests covering all 4 components: brokerBearerMiddleware (RejectsClientSuppliedXCellID + NoBearer + WrongBearer ConstantTime + EmptyExpectedToken + NoBearerNoCellID EC-5 + Concurrent_NoRace), rawSecretReader (HappyPath composite-key + NoRows + NoRows through wrapped EC-8 + TransitDecryptError + PoolNil + TransitNil + Concurrent_NoRace), inlineTenantBinding (HappyPath + TenantNotFound via pgx.ErrNoRows EC-8 follow-up + OtherErrorPropagates + CellStoreNil), setupSecretFetchDeps (5 nil-safe permutations). Minimal DIP refactor (narrow `pgxQuerier` + `tenantCellQuerier` interfaces) gives testability without adding pgxmock (preserved deps-audit PASS_WITH_CAVEATS verdict).
- **`api/internal/k8s/secret_k8s_manager.go` MEDIUM cleanup (closes 5 findings)** — T4.1 replaces hardcoded `fmt.Sprintf("loopzero-%s-%s-%s", ...)` with new `tenantNamespaceFor(...)` wrapper around `naming.Namespace` (per ADR D5: returns `(string, error)`; we wrap-and-propagate via `fmt.Errorf("k8s secret: namespace: %w", err)` instead of silently ignoring — surfacing upstream validation gaps per `feedback_post_feature_live_regression_workflow_2026_06_09`). NEW `buildSecretLabels(tenantID, projectID, app)` helper omits `loopzero.io/app` label when `app == ""` (previously broke selectors of the form `loopzero.io/app=foo` that matched the empty label too). Local `k8sName` var renamed to `secretObjName` so it no longer shadows the package-level `k8sName(...)` function. ExternalSecret CR Update path: EC-4 BOUNDED retry on IsConflict (re-Get + carry fresh resourceVersion + retry ONCE; persistent conflict surfaces wrapped error so caller records `sync_status=failed` — guards against DoS of K8s API). ExternalSecret CR `data.remoteRef` extended with `metadata.{project, environment}` so ESO substitutes them into the broker URL at request time.
- **`api/cmd/setup_secret_fetch.go` pgx canonical error detection (closes 1 MEDIUM + EC-8 absorption)** — replaces fragile `strings.Contains(err.Error(), "no rows")` with `errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows)` in `rawSecretReader.ReadSecret` so the 404 NOT_FOUND mapping survives intermediate `fmt.Errorf("...: %w", err)` wrappers. Parallel EC-8 follow-up landed in `inlineTenantBinding.GetTenantCellID` (closes the parallel fragile `strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not found")` against `db.PostgresCellStore.GetTenantCellID` which wraps pgx.ErrNoRows once).
- **`api/internal/k8s/tenant_cr_manager_test.go` + `api/internal/routes/projects_test.go` (closes 6 pre-existing RED)** — post `2026-06-09-m2-operator-canonical-fix`, the CRD admission requires non-empty `spec.environments[].project` (EC-1) and `EnsureTenant` no longer pre-populates a default `production` env (T4.1 chain appends it). T5.1+T5.2 update 6 RED tests: passes canonical `"testproj"` project name + adds explicit `AddEnvironment(tenantID, "testproj", "production")` calls where tests previously assumed pre-populated production. `TestProjects_Get_Found` fixture swapped from `project: &ProjectResult{...}` (legacy GetByName path) to `projectSummary: []ProjectSummary{...}` (post-T2.1-2026-06-08 ListByTenantWithSummary path).
- **`api/internal/manifests/renderer_test.go` drive-by build unblock** — pre-existing format-verb bug (`%q` against int32 `RollingUpdate.MaxSurge`) fixed to `%d` so `go test ./api/internal/manifests/...` compiles. Required for the Phase 6 integration validation gate (`go test -race ./api/...` must exit 0).


### Security (2026-06-10 LIVE walkthrough — J-22 Layer 2 ESO Sync end-to-end GREEN)

Sponsor LIVE walkthrough on `https://app-dev.loopzero.example` after Cleanroom TOTAL (mandate of 2026-06-10) closed the Layer 2 ESO sync chain end-to-end. 9 distinct bugs were LIVE-caught and shipped in cascade; baseline `docs/operations/baselines/2026-06-10-j22-layer2-eso-sync-live-green.md` records the 4-layer bisect anchor. Sponsor mandate verbatim 2026-06-10: *"Voce deve terminar de implementar, precisamos fechar nosso sistema de Scrects [...] Senao esta fucnionando nao esta implementado"*.

- **`pkg/vault/transit_client.go` hyphen-prefix path + 403/404 self-heal** — OpenBao transit engine rejects `/` inside the key name with 404 *"unsupported path"* (LIVE-caught: `transit/keys/loopzero-tenants/<id>` reachable as root → 200, reachable as AppRole → 403 enumeration-prevention). Fix: `tenantKeyName()` helper returns `loopzero-tenants-<id>` (single-segment hyphen-joined); `tryEncrypt` self-heal trigger broadened from `404` to `404 OR 403` so AppRole-side enumeration-prevention 403s also fire `createTransitKey` (idempotent — true permission denial still surfaces as `ErrTransitKeyCreateFailed` after the create attempt).
- **`docs/operations/openbao-transit-cutover-runbook.md` policy `update` capability** — LIVE-caught: `bao token capabilities` reported `create, read` but actual `PUT /v1/transit/keys/<name>` returned 403 because OpenBao policy-enforces PUT as `update` semantics regardless of pre-existence. Runbook step 1.2 + LIVE policy upload extended to `capabilities = ["create", "read", "update"]`.
- **`api/internal/k8s/secret_k8s_manager.go` M2 3-segment canonical namespace** — LIVE-caught (sponsor secret POST returned `status=created sync_status=failed`): hardcoded `loopzero-{tenantID}-{env}` (M1 2-seg legacy) targeted a namespace that no longer exists post `2026-06-09-m2-operator-canonical-fix`. Now uses `loopzero-{tenantID}-{projectID}-{env}` (3-seg per `pkg/naming.Namespace`). `DeleteSecret` signature extended with `projectID`; `routes/secrets.go:749` call site updated; `domain.SecretK8sSync` interface widened; `routes/secrets_test.go fakeK8sSyncT11` mock signature aligned.
- **`api/internal/k8s/secret_k8s_manager.go` `targetSecretName()` helper** — LIVE-caught: ExternalSecret `spec.target.name = "user-secrets-<t>-<p>-<env>-"` (trailing `-`) violated K8s DNS-1123 grammar when `app` was empty (tenant-wide secrets). Helper omits the empty-app segment so the name stays inside `[a-z0-9]([-a-z0-9]*[a-z0-9])?`.
- **`api/internal/k8s/secret_k8s_manager.go` ExternalSecret CR canonical shape** — `secretStoreRef.name` repointed from legacy `loopzero-mgmt-store` (provider.kubernetes — never deployed) to canonical `loopzero-broker` (provider.webhook). `data.remoteRef` split into two fields `key` + `property` because ESO URL-encodes slashes embedded in a SINGLE `remoteRef.key` (e.g., `tn-X/SECRET` becomes `tn-X%2FSECRET`), which chi router rejects as 404. Two-substitution URL template (`/secret-fetch/{{ .remoteRef.key }}/{{ .remoteRef.property }}`) keeps the slash as a literal path delimiter.
- **Phase 4 broker endpoint wired end-to-end** — `routes/internal_secret_fetch.go` T4.1 handler was shipped weeks ago but never registered. New `api/cmd/setup_secret_fetch.go` composes deps (`rawSecretReader` via PG `QueryRow` + `vault.Transit.Decrypt`; `inlineTenantBinding` via existing `db.PostgresCellStore`; `routes.NewSecretReadAuditAdapter`). `brokerBearerMiddleware` provides constant-time-compare Bearer auth using the shared `loopzero-broker-token` Secret (per `feedback_security_first_mandate`: medium-strong Bearer-via-K8s-Secret hierarchy entry; prod multi-cluster will upgrade to TokenReview when cell ESO ships). `middleware.InjectCrossClusterResultForBroker` helper added so the bearer middleware produces the same context shape the existing handler reads via `CrossClusterFromContext`. Wired in `api/cmd/main.go` (nil-safe — handler stays unset and route is not registered if any dep is missing).
- **`infra/helm/loopzero-external-secrets/templates/clustersecretstore-loopzero-broker.yaml` (NEW)** — `provider.webhook` CSS with two-substitution URL (`{key}/{property}`), `Authorization: Bearer {{ .auth.token }}` templating from `secrets:` block, `X-Cell-ID` header, `result.jsonPath: $.plaintext_b64`. `refreshInterval` integer seconds (ESO v1beta1 requires int; LIVE-caught when string `"1h"` rendered as `ValidationFailed`). `broker.*` values defaults: `enabled=false` (opt-in), `apiURL` defaults to the in-cluster loopzero-api Service.
- **`infra/tilt/api-deployment.yaml` `loopzero-broker-token` envFrom** — loopzero-api mounts `LOOPZERO_BROKER_TOKEN` env var via `envFrom: secretRef: loopzero-broker-token (optional)` next to the existing `loopzero-openbao-approle` mount.
- **`cloud/dashboard/src/pages/secrets.tsx` + 6 sibling files (stash 7cddcbfe restored)** — sponsor LIVE-caught regression: the prior 2026-06-09 fix was stashed instead of committed; Cleanroom rebuilt loopzero-cloud without it. Sheets + Dialogs moved OUTSIDE `<PageShell>` so they mount independent of the empty state slot; `useSecrets({project, env})` shape canonical; `useCreateSecret` routes via `/api/v1/secrets/bulk` with `{project, secrets:[{name,value,envs:[]}]}` shape (avoids singular-`environment` schema mismatch on the legacy POST). Vitest 13/13 GREEN.

Validation: baseline `docs/operations/baselines/2026-06-10-j22-layer2-eso-sync-live-green.md` records all 4 layers GREEN (PG `vault:v1:` 2/2, K8s Secret materialized in tenant ns, broker round-trip 20ms `c2Rmc2FkZmFzZGY=` → `sdfsadfasdf` matches sponsor input, 28 `secret.read.broker` audit entries, ES `SecretSynced True`, 5 controllers Running). Cross-cluster URL via `k3d-loopzero-mgmt-server-0:30001` NodePort works LIVE in dev-public k3d topology; canonical workload-helmfile wire + prod multi-cluster URL templating are open follow-ups. `<promise>IMPLEMENTATION_COMPLETE</promise>` WITHHELD per Unbreakable Rule 3 until sponsor verbatim + T4.1 LIVE 6/6.


### Security (2026-06-10 — J-22 OpenBao Transit + ESO Sync: zero-leak user-secret chain)

Plan `2026-06-10-j22-openbao-transit-eso-sync` v1.2 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 88.4 wires the J-22 dashboard write path through OpenBao transit encryption + declarative ESO sync to workload pod env, absorbing 8 MUST FIX edge cases (EC-01..EC-08). Engineering side closes 18 tasks across 7 phases via 14 commits + 15 ralph-loop iterations. Sponsor mandate verbatim 2026-06-10: *"Pense em nível FAANG PARA QUE NÃO TENHAMOS PROBLEMA DE VAZAMENTO"*. Operator HARD STOPs (helmfile sync + env var flip + T4.1 LIVE 6/6 + sponsor browser walkthrough) remain pending — per per-feature LIVE workflow mandate.

- **`pkg/vault/transit_client.go`** — narrow HTTP client for OpenBao transit with per-tenant key routing, AppRole authentication (EC-04 absorbed via `singleflight.Group.Do("approle-login")` deduplicates concurrent re-login on 403 bursts), SPKI cert pin (refuses imposter cert; NO retry path), and EC-06 absorbed auto-create transit key on 404 + idempotent retry. Per-tenant rate limiter via `golang.org/x/time/rate` + EC-08 absorbed LRU-bounded cache (`golang-lru/v2`, default 10K entries). (T2.1 + EC-06 in commits `7756e21f`, `c021ba87`; T3.4 + EC-08 in commit `caa688ba`)
- **`pkg/vault/zeroize.go`** — EC-07 absorbed: signature accepts ONLY `[]byte` to prevent string-immutability footgun; compiler does not elide writes. (T3.1 in commit `7756e21f`)
- **`pkg/vault/redaction.go`** — slog.Handler wrapper scrubbing `vault:v1:` ciphertext + Vault tokens + long base64 blobs from log records (message + attrs + recurses into groups). Fail-open contract: malformed regex falls through to inner handler. (T3.2 in commit `f0475e58`)
- **`api/internal/routes/secrets.go` Bulk handler** — EC-01 absorbed (continue-best-effort on per-env failure), EC-02 absorbed (`sync_status_per_env: map[string]string` + `partial` aggregate status), EC-03 absorbed (cross-tenant + nonexistent paths return 404 byte-identical per CWE-204 defense; same invariant as Memory Section mandate), T2.3 wires `transit.Encrypt` BEFORE `store.Set` so PG never receives plaintext; transit failure does NOT persist plaintext (defense scan via captured store values). (T1.1 + T1.2 + T2.3 in commits `58fc733c`, `5eaf3ec3`, `c021ba87`)
- **`api/internal/db/secret_wrapper.go` read-path dispatch** — locks the contract via 5 specs (`TestSecretWrapper_RoutesByPrefix` and siblings) + ISP-correct adapter `api/internal/adapters/vault_transit_adapter.go` bridges `pkg/vault.Transit` ↔ `db.VaultTransitClient`. (T2.2 in commit `70e27f19`)
- **`api/internal/config` T2.5 + EC-05 absorbed** — 5 new env vars (`OPENBAO_TRANSIT_ENABLED` + 4 vault credentials/pin). D6 ADR fail-loud refusal at boot when transit enabled but credentials missing. EC-05 boot log emits `crypto_mode=transit|aes-local|plaintext` with `aes_key_ignored` flag so operator sees which mode was chosen (precedence: transit > aes-local > plaintext). (T2.5 in commit `397b8930`)
- **`infra/helm/loopzero-external-secrets/templates/clustersecretstore-openbao-transit.yaml`** — canonical CSS template with AppRole auth + `helm fail` on missing credentials. Hermetic render test 26/26 PASS. (T2.4 in commit `850ba3db`)
- **`infra/helm/loopzero-api/templates/networkpolicy.yaml`** — egress to vault-system on TCP/8200 ONLY. Combined namespaceSelector + podSelector AND semantic (locked against OR-semantic refactor by test C1). Hermetic render test 22/22 PASS. (T3.3 in commit `216cb3a0`)
- **`infra/tests/e2e/dev-public-secrets-zero-leak_test.sh`** — LIVE 6-scenario zero-leak proof per D5 ADR with ephemeral UUID sentinel + EC-14 cleanup trap. OPERATOR HARD STOP. Hermetic syntax companion 38/38 PASS. (T4.1 + T4.2 in commit `0f2ce5c4`)
- **`task test:dogfood:secrets-openbao`** — aggregate wrapper invoking 4 suites: hermetic syntax + pkg/vault race + api routes race + LIVE bash. Hermetic-only `verdict=PASS pass=3 fail=0` in ~120s. (T5.1 + T5.2 in commit `31d798c9`)
- **6 chore(deps) commits** — pre-implementation Path A: go-jose/v4 v4.1.4 (GO-2026-4945), x/net v0.55.0 (6 CVEs), Go directive 1.26 in 8 modules (covers 3 stdlib CVEs), x/sync v0.21.0, terratest v1.0.0 (preparatory). Allowlist 3 stdlib CVEs sunset 2026-07-10. Commits `99b79958..fc185900`.
- **T2.5 boot wire-up closure** — sponsor question 2026-06-10 *"Estamos prontp para fazer o teste live?"* surfaced the deferred engineering gap: the transit client was never constructed at boot, so the LIVE path would have fallen through to legacy AES even with `OPENBAO_TRANSIT_ENABLED=true`. Closed via `api/cmd/setup_transit.go` (NEW — 60 LoC helper that reads `cfg.CryptoMode()` and constructs `*vault.TransitClient` only when mode is `transit`; nil-safe otherwise; D6 ADR fail-loud propagates constructor errors as fatal). `api/cmd/setup_stores.go` extended to thread the optional `vault.Transit` through `setupSecretStore`, branching to `NewPostgresSecretStoreWithVault` via `adapters.NewVaultTransitAdapter` when present. `server.Config.SecretTransit` field + `router.go` `secrets.SetTransitClient(cfg.SecretTransit)` wire injection point. **Anti-double-encrypt defense in `encryptForStore`**: when the Bulk handler (T2.3) pre-encrypts via the same transit client the store also dispatches through, encryptForStore now detects the `vault:v1:` prefix on input and passes through unchanged (no second-trip to OpenBao, no envelope-around-envelope corruption). 9 new specs (4 setup_transit + 3 setup_stores + 2 encryptForStore pass-through) all GREEN. `go build ./... → exit 0`; `golangci-lint --new-from-rev=fbb41362 → 0 issues`.
- **Operator cutover runbook + canonical envFrom wire (iter 20 productive-idle)** — `docs/operations/openbao-transit-cutover-runbook.md` (NEW — 4 HARD STOP steps with exact `vault` + `kubectl` + `helmfile` commands, pre-flight + rollback path + sponsor walkthrough script + post-cutover baseline diff). The chart `infra/helm/loopzero-api/templates/deployment.yaml` envFrom block extended with one new `secretRef: loopzero-openbao-approle` entry (`optional: true` so dev/test boots when secret absent) per `feedback_secret_canonical_no_hardcode`: AppRole secret_id is the ONLY OpenBao credential that MUST come from a K8s Secret (`OPENBAO_VAULT_SECRET_ID`); the 4 non-sensitive env vars (`TRANSIT_ENABLED`, `VAULT_ADDR`, `VAULT_ROLE_ID`, `SPKI_PIN_SHA256`) flow through `.Values.env` as literals. Hermetic render assertion `infra/tests/helm/loopzero-api-openbao-approle-envfrom_test.sh` (NEW — 7/7 PASS) locks the contract against drift via `helm template` + grep, including an ANTI-HARDCODE guard that fails when any inline `value:` appears next to `name: OPENBAO_VAULT_SECRET_ID`. Allowlist entry added.
- **Cleanroom 3-LIVE-catch fix chain (iters 21-22 productive-idle)** — sponsor verbatim *"teste mais agressivo cleanroom (garantindo banco limpo)"* triggered a `task clean:all` + `task setup` + Tilt rebuild cycle that surfaced 3 LIVE-only defects hermetic + go test could NOT catch. Each fixed + committed mid-cleanroom: (a) `aebe2da3` — 8 Dockerfile golang base images bumped `1.25.10 → 1.26.0` to match the post-chore-deps `go 1.26` directive across 9 go.mod files (4-layer drift caught by Docker build under `GOWORK=off` reproducibility); covers `api/Dockerfile` ×2 stages + `cloud/Dockerfile` + 4 operators + `tools/loopzero-dockerfile-lint` + `infra/images/packs-runner` + `pkg/quality/baseimagedigest/check.go` baseline anchor. (b) `902dbd71` — `infra/scripts/local/bootstrap-seed-tenant-cr.sh` aligned with canonical `api/internal/k8s/tenant_cr_manager.go buildTenantCR()` emitting `environments: []` empty list per M2 plan T1.1 `spec.environments[].project: Required value` schema (mirrors anti-hardcode mandate). ### Added (2026-06-09 — J-22 Secrets CRUD: Vercel-canonical UX for env vars)

Plan `2026-06-06-dashboard-journey-j22-secrets-crud` v1.2.1 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 85.6 closes the Secrets page UX gap caught LIVE during the M2 sponsor walkthrough (legacy `cloud/dashboard/src/components/sheets/add-secret-sheet.tsx` had a silent frontend/backend schema mismatch — sent `environments: []` array vs backend `environment: string` — so the Add Secret button surfaced no error but never created the secret). Canonical replacement absorbs 9 of 14 edge cases (EC-13/14/16/17/18/19 + D1/D2/D3/D4) shipping a complete Vercel-canonical UX:

- **POST /api/v1/secrets/bulk** — engine endpoint with `len(results) == len(request.secrets)` invariant (T1.1 / commit `2c1ad46c`). EC-18 size cap 100 → 413 PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE; EC-16 input validation via existing `secretNameRegex`; EC-14 duplicate-key 400 DUPLICATE_KEY_IN_BATCH (T5.2 / commit `f8b66569`); per-key audit log via `LogAudit` (T1.2 / commit `144d7370`); race-safe plan-limit gate via `SecretPlanLimiter` narrow interface returning 402 PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED (T1.3 / commit `aada4635`).
- **useSecrets({env})** hook — env filter wired into queryKey so cache isolates per env (T2.1 / commit `5611a3d2`). Backward-compat: zero-arg call unchanged.
- **4 secret mutations** — `useCreateSecret`, `useRotateSecret` (carries EC-13 `expected_version` for optimistic concurrency), `useDeleteSecret`, `useBulkUploadSecrets` (T2.2 / commit `39a5a586`). Each invalidates `queryKeys.secrets.all` on success.
- **5 canonical components** (T3.1-T3.5 / commits `1147415c`, `0919fd46`, `20a2e91f`, `ef44c84a`, `29bcefd9`): `SecretsTable` enforces D1 masking forever by construction (no plaintext API); `AddSecretSheet` with D4 env multi-select replaces legacy schema-mismatched sheet; `RotateSecretDialog` + `DeleteSecretDialog` with D3 type-to-confirm + EC-19 case-sensitive paste; `BulkUploadSheet` with browser-side dotenv parser + preview.
- **dotenv parser** (`cloud/dashboard/src/lib/dotenv.ts`) — pure function handling EC-17 quoted values + comment lines + EC-14 duplicate-key pre-flight (T3.5).
- **Page integration** (T4.1 / commit `67921a9d`) — legacy `cloud/dashboard/src/components/sheets/add-secret-sheet.tsx` DELETED. `secrets.tsx` net `-104 LOC` after canonical wire. URL `?env=` filter source-of-truth (T4.2 / commit `b979b044`).
- **Test coverage**: 35 vitest specs / 9 files (T5.1 / commit `38a25350`) + 5 Go specs covering all T1.1/T1.2/T1.3/EC-18/EC-14 acceptance scenarios + LIVE bash 4 scenarios (T5.3 / commit `b04dd8d9`) + hermetic syntax 23/0 PASS (T5.4 / commit `5c51d8fd`) + aggregate dogfood wrapper (T6.1 / commit `4871caa7`) + `task test:dogfood:secrets` taskfile entry (T6.2 / commit `0cf1cc23`).
- **Known follow-ups (NOT blocking J-22 engineering closure)**: EC-9 (per-key savepoint atomicity), EC-10 (`pg_advisory_xact_lock` race-safe gate via existing `SecretEnvLocker`), EC-11 (ESO `SecretSynced=True` polling), EC-12 (fail-loud audit transactionality), dedicated rotate-with-version endpoint (currently re-POST upsert serves as rotate path; useRotateSecret PUT contract is shipped on frontend awaiting backend route). LIVE bash + sponsor browser walkthrough = operator HARD STOPs per per-feature mandate `feedback_post_feature_live_regression_workflow_2026_06_09`.


### Changed (2026-06-09 — M2 operator canonical fix: end-to-end M2 namespace alignment)

Plan `2026-06-09-m2-operator-canonical-fix` v1.4 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 89.0 closes the M2 operator drift documented in T6.1 of plan `2026-06-08-pre-release-test-rigor`. The manifest renderer (`api/internal/manifests/`) already emitted the M2 canonical 3-segment namespace `loopzero-<tenant>-<project>-<env>` since plan `2026-06-07-runtime-envs-per-project-m2`, but TenantController + EnvironmentController were still emitting the M1 2-segment `loopzero-<tenant>-<env>`. Production deploys cascaded `AppProject reject destination → namespace not found → SA not found → pods Forbidden`, requiring 4 manual operator workarounds per deploy (memory `feedback_m2_canonical_drift_operator_renderer`). Post-fix, the operator emits M2 canonical end-to-end:

- **TenantController.reconcileArgoCDApp** — emits AppProject `spec.destinations` from the per-project (Project, EnvName) Cartesian product. Silent M1 fallback removed; empty Project is now a contract violation. (T2.1 + T2.2 in commits `1afce5b7`, `c36a7ad9`)
- **EnvironmentController.reconcileNamespace** — computes canonical M2 namespace from `Spec.Project + Spec.Environment` instead of trusting `Spec.Namespace.Name`. EC-3 absorption: 63-char overflow sets `TypeEnvNamespaceReady=False Reason=NameTooLong` and returns `ctrl.Result{}` without requeue. (T3.1 in commit `1a014d11`)
- **TenantEnvironmentEntry.Project** — required at CRD level with RFC 1123 Pattern + MinLength=1. Eliminates the pre-#69 backwards-compat path that allowed the silent M1 fallback. Phase ordering per ADR D5: T5.1 migration ships BEFORE T1.1 CRD tightens to avoid existing-CR stuck-loop. (T1.1 in commit `566613ee`)
- **POST /api/v1/projects** — auto-creates the `production` environment via the existing `services.EnvironmentEnsurer` (ADR D4 Vercel-style: every project has production from day 1). Reuses the same instance wired by the build handler — single source of truth. (T4.1 in commit `5d47a7ca`)
- **migrate-legacy-namespaces.sh** — dual-mode (`--mode={delete|adopt}` per ADR D3) with EC-6 PVC safety gate (`--include-pvcs=false` default; opt-in requires `--pv-rebind-strategy={retain|clone}` and points to `docs/operations/pv-rebind-runbook.md`) + EC-7 active-release precondition (refuses `--mode=adopt` when any `releases.status` IN (PENDING, ROLLING_BACK)). (T5.1 in commit `7c18a8a5`)
- **dev-public-production-deploy_test.sh** — LIVE 13-step E2E test against `dev-public` with 4 anti-workaround grep guards enforced via 30-assertion hermetic structural test. Canonical-by-construction guarantee: any future PR that reintroduces a manual operator workaround breaks the T6.1 hermetic test on CI. (T6.1 in commit `ba8e90db`)
- **Steady-state baselines** — pre-fix (`docs/operations/baselines/2026-06-09-pre-m2-operator-fix.md`) + post-fix (`docs/operations/baselines/2026-06-09-post-m2-operator-fix.md`) bookend the milestone per `rules/steady-state-baseline.md`.
- **Known follow-ups (NOT blocking the M2 fix delivery)**: F1 — 10 tenant_integration_test M1 expectations need M2 alignment sweep. F2 — strict EC-5 PG-tx-first/K8s-after rollback on POST /api/v1/projects requires Store API surgery; deferred. F5 — LIVE E2E + sponsor browser walkthrough = operator HARD STOPs for `IMPLEMENTATION_COMPLETE` promise.


### Changed (2026-06-07 — M2 Runtime Environments per-project canonical refactor)

Per plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-07-runtime-envs-per-project-m2-plan.md` v1.2 (sponsor verbatim 2026-06-07: *"Continue evoluindo o plano ate que ele passe"*). 17 of 22 plan tasks complete this session; the 3 deferred dashboard tasks (T5.1/2/3) have ready backend contracts and ship in a follow-up cycle. Phase 9 dogfood + sponsor LIVE browser test on dev-public remain operator-action HARD STOPs.

- `POST /api/v1/environments` now REQUIRES `project` in the JSON body (slug, not UUID per "Project name vs project ID" convention). Missing project → 400 `MISSING_FIELD`. Cross-tenant project leak gate (EC-1 SEPA-CRITICAL) enforced via `EnvProjectLookup.ProjectExistsForTenant(tenant_id, name)` — a project belonging to another tenant returns 404 `PROJECT_NOT_FOUND`, never silent 201. Duplicate (project, env) tuple → 409 `ENV_ALREADY_EXISTS` (not 201). Reserved name `production` now tenant-wide cross-project (EC-8).
- `DELETE /api/v1/environments/{name}?project=<slug>` filters by `(name, project)` tuple — removing `staging+web` no longer touches `staging+api`. Response includes `warnings[]` array when the env still has an active release (EC-7 orphan-App warning pointing user at `loopzero platform status`).
- `GET /api/v1/environments/{name}?project=<slug>` optional query parameter resolves the canonical (name, project) tuple; legacy callers (no `?project=`) still match production-legacy entries (ADR D2).
- `GET /api/v1/environments` response: each entry of `environments[]` now carries `project` field (omitempty preserves legacy production shape — EC-9 disclosure for strict-typed clients).
- `EnvironmentManager` interface (`AddEnvironment`, `RemoveEnvironment`, `GetEnvironmentDetail`) carries `project` end-to-end. The Tenant CR spec.environments entry is populated with the project field needed by the operator-side canonical namespace `loopzero-<tenant>-<project>-<env>` (pkg/naming.Namespace).
- `EnvironmentEnsurer.EnsureEnvironment(ctx, tenant, project, env)` propagates project through Lister/Adder/ReadinessChecker. `EnvironmentCRReader.IsReady` looks up the canonical CR name `<tenant>-<project>-<env>` when project is non-empty; legacy `<tenant>-<env>` lookup preserved when project="" (ADR D2).
- `loopzero env create <name> --project <project-slug>` CLI flag REQUIRED post-M2. Missing or whitespace-only `--project` → exit 1 with `MISSING_FIELD` and actionable hint.
- TenantCRManager `AddEnvironment`: idempotency check is per-(name, project) tuple so `staging+web` and `staging+api` coexist as 2 distinct entries in spec.environments. EC-4 pre-flight guard rejects combined namespace > 63 chars before the apiserver round-trip via pkg/naming.Namespace validation.
- `plan_limits` table semantic change via migration `000032_plan_caps_m2`: the `environments` row is reinterpreted as per-(project, env) tuple cap (NOT tenant-wide aggregate). Cap values locked per ADR D4: Starter=1, Pro=5, Team=20 envs-per-project. Grandfather strategy (T0.2 sponsor sign-off): existing over-cap tuples preserved, new writes gated by new cap. **NOTE on numbering:** plan body cited migration 000029 but post-merge with origin/develop the memory migrations (000027-29 renamed to 000029-31, commit `aa895b27`) shifted available numbers — M2 takes the next free slot 000032 (SEPA CRITICAL #1).
- `pkg/enforcement.CountPermanentEnvironments` query changed from `COUNT(DISTINCT environment)` to `COUNT(*)` over (tenant, project, env) PK — pre-M2 DISTINCT was an under-count workaround when env_targets lacked project segment; post-M2 PK guarantees per-tuple uniqueness.

LIVE smoke harness: `infra/tests/e2e/dev-public-runtime-env-flow_test.sh` (4 scenarios A/B/C/D against `cloud-dev.loopzero.example`) + hermetic structural test `infra/tests/check-dev-public-runtime-env-flow-syntax_test.sh` (20/20 PASS). Both registered in shell-scripts-allowlist.

Dashboard surface (Phase 5):
- `CreateEnvironmentSheet` now picks a project via Select dropdown. EC-2 empty-state Alert + "Create a project →" CTA when 0 projects exist; auto-fill + read-only when exactly 1 project (the common Free/Starter shape); manual pick when ≥2 projects. POST body carries the `project` field aligned with T4.1.
- `/environments` page groups environments by project. Search matches across both name AND project (`web` surfaces every env in project=web; `staging` surfaces every staging across projects; `web/staging` matches the exact tuple). Legacy entries (no project) render under a dedicated "production (legacy)" group at the top with a "— no project" indicator (ADR D6 + ADR D2 backward compat).
- `DeleteEnvironmentDialog` accepts a `project` prop and propagates it to the DELETE URL as `?project=<slug>`. The hook `useDeleteEnvironment` now accepts `{ name, project }` shape (backward-compatible with legacy string-only callers). Removing `staging+web` no longer touches `staging+api`.

Vitest aggregator (T8.2): full dashboard suite 533/542 PASS (8 skipped pre-existing, 1 pre-existing failure in `lib/mocks/handlers.test.ts > test_build_sbom_returns_cyclonedx` unrelated to M2 — confirmed via git-stash to be identical on the pre-M2 base). Zero new failures introduced by Phase 5.


### Fixed (2026-06-08 — M2 EC-1 cross-tenant gate LIVE wiring)

- LIVE bash test caught that the EC-1 SEPA-CRITICAL cross-tenant project gate (T4.1) was unwired in production: `setup_server.go` constructed `EnvironmentHandler` with `WithKPILookup` + `WithPreviewSlotLookup` but NEVER called `.WithProjectLookup(...)`. The handler fell open (test-fixture nil-bypass branch), so the LIVE first run accepted `project=definitely-not-mine-xyz-foreign` with HTTP 201 — exactly the security bug the gate was designed to prevent.
- New `api/cmd/env_lookups_adapter.go`: thin adapter wrapping the canonical `routes.ProjectStore` (PostgresProjectStore) and exposing the smaller `EnvProjectLookup` interface (ISP). `GetByName` already scopes by `tenant_id = $1 AND name = $2` (queries/projects.sql), so the adapter is a pure nil-check pass-through.
- `setup_server.go`: `.WithProjectLookup(&envProjectLookupAdapter{store: dbDeps.ProjectStore})` wired into the handler. Production paths now hit the gate.
- LIVE re-run post-fix: 4/4 scenarios GREEN (A bootstrap JWT × cross-tenant project → 404 PROJECT_NOT_FOUND; B missing project → 400; C ?project= honored; D foreign project → 404). Tenant CR captured with `"project":"web"` in spec.environments entry — Phase 2 T2.1 LIVE-validated end-to-end.


### Changed (2026-06-07 — Preview as Category single-slot model — Netlify-style)

Per plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-07-preview-as-category-single-slot-plan.md` (sponsor mandate verbatim 2026-06-07: *"Implemente assim: 'Preview' (1 entry). Somente um Preview ativo nao por branch."*). Engineering side phases 0-7 + Phase 8 test scripts shipped; LIVE dogfood + sponsor browser test are operator-action HARD STOPs.

**5 latent bugs closed (Bugs A-E in the plan's Coverage Matrix):**

- **Bug A — enforcement counted previews**: pre-2026-06-07 the inline COUNT query `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM environment_targets WHERE tenant_id = $1` mixed permanent + preview rows. A Pro tenant with 1 prod env + 2 preview-pr-N rows hit `current=3, max=3` PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED even though only 1 was a real permanent env. Fix: migration 000027 adds `kind` column (CHECK 'permanent'|'preview'); `pkg/enforcement/CountPermanentEnvironments` filters by `kind='permanent'`; `api/internal/server/router.go` envEnforcement switches to the new helper.
- **Bug B — dashboard hid previews**: `/api/v1/environments` returned only `{environments[]}`; dashboard had no row for the active preview deployment. Fix: API expand-contract evolution to `{environments[], permanent[], preview{}}` (legacy field preserved for back-compat); new `PreviewRow` + `EnvKindBadge` components in `cloud/dashboard/src/components/environments/`.
- **Bug C — cap consistency**: schema-level enforcement of "one active preview per tenant". Fix: new `preview_slot` table with `tenant_id` PRIMARY KEY + `preview_slot_all_or_nothing` CHECK constraint. ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE forces atomic substitution (D3).
- **Bug D — cleanup chain**: closing a PR didn't clean up the `environment_targets` row or the slot. Fix: `PreviewService.DeletePreviewByPR` extended with `SlotStore.Clear` + `EnvTargets.DeleteByKind` (best-effort, idempotent).
- **Bug E — DB out-of-sync with K8s**: `container_delivery.activateRelease` always wrote `kind='permanent'` for any env. Fix: canonical `naming.IsPreviewEnv("preview-pr-N")` detector + dispatch to `SetPreviewDesired` (kind='preview').

**Schema migration 000027 (LIVE-applied):**

- `environment_targets ADD COLUMN kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'permanent' CHECK IN ('permanent', 'preview')`
- `CREATE INDEX idx_environment_targets_kind ON environment_targets (tenant_id, kind)`
- `CREATE TABLE preview_slot (tenant_id text PRIMARY KEY, project_id, active_pr_number, active_branch, active_release_id REFERENCES releases(id) ON DELETE SET NULL, url, updated_at)` with `preview_slot_all_or_nothing` CHECK + RLS policy mirroring `environment_targets`.
- **Clean break (D8)**: `DELETE FROM environment_targets WHERE environment LIKE 'preview-pr-%'` + `DELETE FROM preview_environments WHERE pr_number IS NOT NULL`. Existing K8s-side preview namespaces + ArgoCD Apps cleaned up by Phase 0 T0.2 operator runbook.

**Honest disclosures (Phase 8 gates open):**

1. **LIVE dogfood run** — `infra/tests/e2e/dev-public-preview-flow_test.sh` extended with Scenarios F (single-slot SoT) + G (D5 kind separation). Hermetic syntax test 23/23 PASS. LIVE run against `dev-public` is operator action.
2. **Sponsor manual browser test** — `/environments` dashboard render with preview row + permanent env create until cap is sponsor's HARD STOP.
3. **`/dogfood full` health score >= 70** — operator action against full LIVE cluster.

**Wiring triad evidence per commit:**

- Pillar (a) caller: `cmd/setup_stores.go` registers `PreviewSlotStore`; `cmd/setup_server.go` wires `previewSlotLookupAdapter` into `EnvironmentHandler`; `cmd/setup_github.go` + `cmd/setup_server.go` pass new deps to `PreviewService`.
- Pillar (b) integration test: `pkg/enforcement/counters_test.go` (4 sqlmock cases pinning canonical query); `api/internal/services/preview_service_test.go` (3 new cases for D3 substitute + D6 cleanup); `cloud/dashboard/src/components/environments/preview-row.test.tsx` (4 cases for active vs empty state).
- Pillar (c) runtime metric: deferred per plan global DoD — `preview_slot.active_pr_number observed non-NULL after PR open, NULL after PR close, in real LIVE workflow` is the operator's LIVE evidence gate.


### Added (2026-06-06 — Delivery Replay — closes Memory Webhook Delivery OOS #5)

Per `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-delivery-replay-plan.md` v1.0. Single-session standalone plan that closes the explicit Out of Scope #5 of the Memory Webhook Delivery plan ("Customer-triggered manual replay of failed deliveries"). Manual re-trigger endpoint + per-row Replay button surfaced inside the recently-promoted DeliveriesSheet. Composition: builds on the worker + state machine + deliveries-listing shipped by Memory Webhook Delivery.

- **`POST /v1/memory/webhooks/{webhook_id}/deliveries/{delivery_id}/replay` handler** at `cloud/internal/routes/memory_webhook_deliveries.go::Replay` (NEW receiver) — branches on the delivery's current `status`: `delivered` → 400 `ALREADY_DELIVERED` (prevent duplicate dispatch to subscriber); `pending|delivering` → 200 idempotent no-op (worker already has it queued); `failed|dead_letter` → 200 with state reset (`status='pending'`, `attempt_count=0`, `next_attempt_at=NOW()`, `last_response_status=NULL`, `last_error_message=NULL`) re-arming the worker's full retry budget. Tenant-scoped via SQL JOIN to `memory_webhooks` (`subject = $2 AND tenant_id = $3`) — cross-tenant + nonexistent both collapse to 404 (P0 D5 existence-leak invariant). Path-param `webhook_id` cross-checked against `deliveries.webhook_id` to defeat URL fishing.
- **Route registered** at `cloud/cmd/main.go` — `r.Post("/webhooks/{webhook_id}/deliveries/{delivery_id}/replay", ...)` inside the existing `/v1/memory` block under `RequireScope("memory:admin")`. Distinct chi param names (`webhook_id` / `delivery_id`) match the handler's `chi.URLParam` reads.
- **Dashboard Replay button + ConfirmDialog** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory/webhooks.tsx` (EDIT) — `RecentDeliveriesBadge` Badge becomes a clickable trigger for the new `DeliveriesSheet` right-side drawer; the sheet lazy-fetches `/v1/memory/webhooks/{id}/deliveries` and renders each as a `DeliveryCard`. Cards with `status ∈ {failed, dead_letter}` get a "Replay" Button (RotateCw icon). Click → `ConfirmDialog` with `confirmationPhrase="replay"` typed-verification + description showing event_type + last error context. On confirm POST → success toast (distinguishes "Replay queued" from "Delivery already queued" via the `noop` field in the response) → invalidate `deliveries` query. Toast on failure.
- **Tests** — 8 new Go handler test groups (17 sub-cases total) covering: unauthenticated 401; malformed `webhook_id` 400 BEFORE DB (5-input sub-table); malformed `delivery_id` 400 BEFORE DB (6-input sub-table including `mwh-` prefix as wrong family); cross-tenant 404 NOT 403 (D5); webhook_id mismatch 404; `delivered` 400 `ALREADY_DELIVERED`; `pending|delivering` 200 no-op (sub-table; UPDATE NOT executed); `failed|dead_letter` 200 reset (sub-table; UPDATE executed with the 5-column reset signature).
- **Invariants verified:** Invariant 8 (grep paper-names in changed files → 0 hits); Invariant 6 (this loop did not modify OSS sibling `loopzero-memory/examples/chat-with-memory/`); Rule 3 (no internal terms in customer-facing strings — uses `delivery`, `replay`, `re-queue`, `subscriber`).
- **Governance gate** at `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` — appended `## Approved Scope Extension — Delivery Replay (sponsor-direct 2026-06-06)`. No migration scope needed (reuses existing `memory_webhook_deliveries` columns from `000030`, renumbered from `000028` on 2026-06-07).

**Honest disclosures (Out of Scope at v0.1):**
- Bulk replay (replay ALL failed deliveries for a webhook in one click) — defer until customer asks
- Replay history audit log entry — defer; current invariant is the dashboard `deliveries` list itself reflects the new state
- Per-delivery exponential-backoff override (e.g., "replay now, ignore the next_attempt_at delay") — current implementation sets `next_attempt_at=NOW()` so worker picks it up on the very next poll


### Added (2026-06-06 — API Key Lineage View — closes API Key Rotation OOS #4)

Per `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-api-key-lineage-view-plan.md` v1.0. Single-session standalone plan that closes the explicit Out of Scope #4 of the API Key Rotation plan ("Customer-facing rotation history view (`GET /v1/api-keys/{id}/lineage`)"). Pure-read endpoint + Sheet drawer UI showing the full rotation chain as a vertical timeline. Composition: requires the `rotated_from` column shipped in API Key Rotation (000031, renumbered from 000029 on 2026-06-07) — impossible without it.

- **`GET /v1/api-keys/{id}/lineage` handler** at `cloud/internal/routes/api_keys.go::LineageKey` (EDIT) — single recursive CTE walks both ancestors (via `rotated_from` backward) and descendants (via reverse FK forward), UNION-ed and ORDER BY created_at ASC (D1: root first → current last). Each item carries `{id, name, key_prefix, created_at, revoked_at, expires_at, is_current}`. `is_current` computed server-side: `revoked_at IS NULL AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > NOW())`. D2 defensive LIMIT 100 caps pathological chains. D3 cross-tenant via `WHERE subject = $2 AND tenant_id = $3` on every recursive arm + the empty-result→404 collapse (preserves P0 D5 existence-leak invariant). EC-2-style malformed id rejected with 400 BEFORE DB query.
- **Route registered** at `cloud/cmd/main.go` — `r.Get("/{id}/lineage", ...)` inside the existing `/v1/api-keys` block under `requireJWT` (D5 / D8 P0 dispatch). No new scope needed; dashboard-only consumer.
- **Dashboard Lineage button + Sheet drawer** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/api-keys.tsx` (EDIT) — `History` icon button per row between Rotate + Trash. Click → opens right-side `<Sheet>` drawer (no z-index collision with the centered Rotate/Delete confirm dialogs). Sheet body lazy-fetches `/v1/api-keys/{id}/lineage` via TanStack `useQuery` + renders a vertical timeline of `<LineageRow>` cards. Each row: key_prefix mask + name + relative created_at + status `Badge` (Active=success / Expired=warning / Revoked=destructive) + an additional "Current" Badge highlighting the row where `is_current=true`. The current row also gets a colored border via `border-primary`. New helper functions `LineageSheet`, `LineageRow` defined inline (Rule of 3 — single consumer).
- **Tests** — 6 new Go handler tests + 1 new vitest case:
  - Unauthenticated → 401
  - 6-input malformed id → 400 BEFORE DB (sub-table)
  - Cross-tenant → 404 NOT 403 (D5)
  - Single-key (no rotation) → 1 item with `is_current=true`
  - 4-rotation chain → 4 items root-first, current-last, exactly one `is_current=true`
  - DB error → 500
  - Vitest: Lineage button opens Sheet; timeline renders both items; `is_current=true` row has the Current badge; `is_current=false` row does NOT
- **Invariants verified:** Invariant 8 (grep paper-names → 0 hits); Invariant 6 (this loop did not modify OSS sibling); Rule 3 (no internal terms in customer-facing strings).
- **Governance gate** at `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` — appended `## Approved Scope Extension — API Key Lineage View (sponsor-direct 2026-06-06)`. No migration scope needed (uses existing 000031 column, renumbered from 000029 on 2026-06-07).

**Honest disclosures (Out of Scope at v0.1):**
- Operator/admin "lineage across all customers" view — would need new admin scope
- Lineage export (CSV/JSON download) — defer until customer asks
- Lineage rotation reasons / notes column — would need new schema migration


### Added (2026-06-06 — API Key Rotation — closes Plan 0 D6 deferral)

Per `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-api-key-rotation-plan.md` v1.0. Standalone plan AFTER Memory Webhook Delivery, closing the explicit honesty debt registered in Plan 0 ADR D6: "Rotation flow (overlap window) is DEFERRED to v1.x after telemetry shows customer pain". Customer can now rotate an API key with zero-downtime overlap, get RFC 8594 Sunset/Deprecation/Link headers on responses authenticated by an expiring key, and audit which key replaced which via the new `rotated_from` column.

- **Migration 000031_loopzero_api_keys_rotated_from** (renumbered from 000029 on 2026-06-07 after merge with preview branch) — `ALTER TABLE loopzero_api_keys ADD COLUMN rotated_from TEXT REFERENCES loopzero_api_keys(id)` (nullable). Populated when a key is created via `POST /v1/api-keys/{id}/rotate`; NULL when created directly via `POST /v1/api-keys`. Self-FK ensures the audit chain cannot dangle. COMMENT ON COLUMN documents the semantics for the next operator reading the schema.
- **`POST /v1/api-keys/{id}/rotate` handler** at `cloud/internal/routes/api_keys.go::RotateKey` — single-transaction rotation per ADR D1: SELECT FOR UPDATE the old row (must exist, belong to authenticated subject+tenant, not revoked, not already-expired), INSERT a new row with `rotated_from = old.id` + same scopes + fresh `loopzero_live_*` secret + apk-{16hex} id + name suffixed `(rotated YYYY-MM-DD)`, UPDATE the old row's `expires_at = NOW() + grace_window`, COMMIT. Returns 200 with new key id + secret (shown ONCE per Plan 0 D3) + `old_expires_at` + `grace_window_hours` for the dashboard to display. Optional body `{"grace_window_hours": N}` per D2: range 1-168 (default 24); out-of-range → 400 `INVALID_GRACE_WINDOW`. Uses `*int` for the body field so an explicit `0` is distinguishable from absent (Go zero-value gotcha). Cross-tenant rotation, revoked-key rotation, already-expired-key rotation all collapse to 404 NOT 403 (D5 P0 existence-leak invariant). Malformed id format → 400 BEFORE DB query (EC-2 pattern from P0).
- **Auth middleware Sunset+Deprecation+Link headers** at `cloud/internal/auth/scope.go::RequireScope` — when an authenticated key's `expires_at IS NOT NULL` AND `expires_at < NOW() + 7 days`, the response carries: `Sunset: <RFC 1123 timestamp>` + `Deprecation: true` + `Link: </docs/api-keys#rotation>; rel="deprecation"`. RFC 8594 standard. Well-written consumer SDKs (Stripe, OpenAI, Anthropic patterns) can subscribe and auto-migrate. Threshold constant `sunsetWarningThreshold = 7 * 24 * time.Hour`. Headers absent when `expires_at IS NULL` OR `expires_at > NOW() + 7d`. ZERO extra DB queries — the existing key lookup now SELECTs `expires_at` alongside the other columns; threshold check is in-memory time arithmetic.
- **D5 invariant honored — no new dispatcher needed** for post-grace cleanup. The existing RequireScope middleware already filters `expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > NOW()` in the lookup query (per Plan 0 D6). After the grace window passes, the old key naturally 401s. Audit history preserved as a row in the DB (revoked_at remains NULL; expires_at went into the past).
- **Dashboard Rotate button** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/api-keys.tsx` (EDIT) — Recycle icon per row alongside the existing Trash icon. Click → `ConfirmRotateDialog` with title `Rotate API key "<name>"?` + body explaining the 24h grace window + `confirmationPhrase={key.name}` typed verification + `intent="destructive"` (rotation IS a destructive operation per design system). On success: reuse the existing `RevealedSecretDialog` (P0 show-once contract) to display the NEW key's secret. Zero new component needed; full reuse of the P0 reveal pattern with its Checkbox-gated Close.
- **Routes registered** in `cloud/cmd/main.go` — `POST /v1/api-keys/{id}/rotate` wired into the existing `/v1/api-keys/*` route group under `requireJWT` (D8 dispatch from Plan 0).
- **Tests** — 11 new Go tests (sub-tables push effective count higher) + 5 vitest aggregate (3 P0 baseline + 2 new Rotate):
  - 5 migration 000031 structural tests (ALTER + ADD COLUMN + REFERENCES + COMMENT + forward-only; renumbered from 000029 on 2026-06-07)
  - 8 RotateKey handler tests: unauthenticated → 401; happy path default grace; happy path grace 1/24/72/168 (sub-table); out-of-range grace (0/-1/169/9999) → 400; cross-tenant → 404; revoked → 404; already-expired → 404; 6-input malformed id → 400 BEFORE DB
  - 3 new RequireScope middleware tests: Sunset header absent when expires_at NULL; absent when expires_at > 7d future; present + RFC 1123 parseable when expires_at within 7d (+ Deprecation: true + Link rel="deprecation")
  - 2 new vitest cases: Rotate button opens ConfirmDialog with grace-window copy; successful rotation triggers show-once secret reveal in the existing P0 RevealedSecretDialog
- **Invariants verified:** Invariant 8 (grep paper-names over P6 new/edited files → 0 hits); Invariant 6 (this loop did not modify OSS sibling); Rule 3 (no internal terms in customer-facing strings).
- **Governance gate** at `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` — appended `## Approved Scope Extension — API Key Rotation (sponsor-direct 2026-06-06)`. Migration path included from the start.

**Honest disclosures (Out of Scope at v0.1):**

- Bulk rotate (admin "rotate all team keys") — future plan
- Auto-rotation policy (rotate every 90d) — future plan; requires opt-in tier
- `apikey.rotation.scheduled` webhook event — future plan (would compose with the Webhook Delivery dispatcher just shipped)
- Customer-facing rotation history view (`GET /v1/api-keys/{id}/lineage` listing all keys in the rotation chain) — future plan
- Per-plan-tier max grace_window cap — future plan
- The `pages.smoke.test.ts` flake (test timed out in 5000ms cold-importing `overview.tsx` when run in full aggregate) is **pre-existing** — passes when the file is run in isolation. NOT a regression introduced by this plan; will be addressed by a separate "raise testTimeout for the smoke test" plan.


### Added (2026-06-06 — Memory Webhook Delivery — closes Plan 3 D2 honesty debt)

Per `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-memory-webhook-delivery-plan.md` v1.0. Standalone plan AFTER the Dashboard Memory section chain (P0-P4) TERMINAL. Ships the dispatcher + signing + retry + dead-letter path that lets registered memory webhooks actually fire. With this entry, the explicit "Demo only" Alert disclosure registered as Plan 3 ADR D2 is honestly retired.

- **Migration 000029_memory_webhooks_add_secret** (renumbered from 000027 on 2026-06-07 after merge with preview branch; 000027/000028 slots LIVE in PG via preview_as_category + preview_slot_per_project) — ALTER TABLE `memory_webhooks` ADD COLUMN `secret TEXT` (nullable). Pre-this-plan webhooks (Plan 3 era) keep NULL; the dispatcher logs a warning and skips signing for those. Future migration NOT-NULL once telemetry shows all pre-plan webhooks revoked. COMMENT ON COLUMN documents the v0.1 semantics for the next operator who reads the schema.
- **Migration 000030_memory_webhook_deliveries** (renumbered from 000028 on 2026-06-07) — NEW table per Plan D3: id PK + webhook_id FK CASCADE + event_type + payload jsonb + status CHECK 5 literals (`pending|delivering|delivered|failed|dead_letter`) + attempt_count + next_attempt_at + last_response_status/body/error + timestamps. Partial index `(next_attempt_at) WHERE status IN ('pending','failed')` for the dispatcher hot path; composite `(webhook_id, created_at DESC)` for the dashboard history view.
- **`POST /v1/memory/webhooks` returns `secret` per D1** at `cloud/internal/routes/memory_webhooks.go` (EDIT) — Create handler now generates 32 random bytes via `crypto/rand`, base64url-encodes them (RawURLEncoding), prepends `whsec_` → 49-char total secret. Persisted raw via the 7th `INSERT` arg. Returned ONCE in the create response (Stripe show-once pattern; parallel to Plan 0 D3 API key reveal). Customer is responsible for storing it to verify HMAC signatures.
- **HMAC-SHA256 signing** at `cloud/internal/memory_webhook_worker/signing.go` (NEW) — `Sign(secret, ts, body) string` returns hex; `HeaderValue(ts, hmac) string` returns Stripe-pattern `t={ts},v1={hex}`. Plan D1 invariant: HMAC covers `{ts}.{body}` — bound to both the content AND a specific moment in time (replay defense when the consumer enforces a window). 5 signing-vector tests prove Stripe-compatibility + key-binding + body-binding + timestamp-binding.
- **Exponential backoff retry** at `cloud/internal/memory_webhook_worker/retry.go` (NEW) — `NextBackoff(attempt) Duration` returns `60s * 2^(attempt-1)` with ±20% jitter; `MaxAttempts=9`. Total worst-case window without jitter: 60+120+240+480+960+1920+3840+7680 = 15300s ≈ 4.25h. After 9th attempt the row terminal-transitions to `dead_letter`. 6 retry tests cover all 8 backoff windows + jitter bounds + invalid-attempt defensive return + IsTerminalAttempt sanity + total-window math + poll-interval cadence guard.
- **Dispatcher + worker** at `cloud/internal/memory_webhook_worker/dispatcher.go` + `worker.go` (NEW) — `Worker` struct with `Run(ctx)` poll loop every 5 seconds + `MaxInFlight=10` semaphore-bounded concurrency + graceful shutdown via context. `Dispatch(ctx, httpClient, logger, delivery)` does ONE HTTP attempt: signs (when secret non-NULL), sets `Idempotency-Key: delivery-{id}` per D5, sets `X-LoopZero-Event: {event_type}` for fast consumer routing, captures the response status + body (truncated 4 KiB) for audit, and returns a DispatchResult that the worker persists. `claimDue()` uses `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` so a future multi-replica deployment can naturally race-safely (v0.1 is single-replica per D4 — multi-replica leader election deferred). 8 dispatcher tests cover: 2xx → delivered; 5xx → failed + ErrorMessage; Idempotency-Key stable across retries (D5 critical); X-LoopZero-Signature HMAC verified via independent re-computation; NULL secret → signature OMITTED (D1 consequence); X-LoopZero-Event header; 4 KiB body truncation; Idempotency-Key stability across multiple Dispatch invocations of the same delivery_id.
- **Event-publishing hook** at `cloud/internal/routes/memory.go` (EDIT) — successful `Forget` now invokes `eventPublisher.Publish(ctx, subject, tenantID, "memory.forgotten", {memory_id})` for fan-out. New `WebhookEventPublisher` interface in routes package + production implementation `MemoryWebhookEventPublisher` at `cloud/internal/routes/memory_webhook_deliveries.go` (NEW) that finds matching active webhooks via `events ?| jsonb` containment + INSERTs one delivery row each. Failures are logged but NOT propagated — the cross-cut fan-out must NEVER regress the primary mutation's success/failure status. 3 forget-event tests: emit on success; no emit on NotFound (mutation didn't happen); no panic when publisher nil (dev mode).
- **`GET /v1/memory/webhooks/{id}/deliveries`** at `cloud/internal/routes/memory_webhook_deliveries.go::List` — dashboard history endpoint. Returns last 10 deliveries scoped to authenticated `(subject, tenant_id)` via JOIN on `memory_webhooks`. Cross-tenant attempts yield empty result (D5 inheritance from Plan 0 — never 403). Registered in `cloud/cmd/main.go` under `auth.RequireScope("memory:admin")`. ID validated against `^mwh-[0-9a-f]{16}$` regex BEFORE DB query. Error message truncated + control-char-stripped before serialization to the wire.
- **Dispatcher worker started in `cloud/cmd/main.go`** alongside the existing metering scheduler — shares `schedulerCtx` for graceful shutdown on SIGTERM (worker drains in-flight dispatches before exiting Run).
- **Dashboard `/memory/webhooks` page** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory/webhooks.tsx` (EDIT):
  - **REMOVED** the persistent `<Alert intent="warning" title="Demo only">` block from Plan 3 D2 (EC-14). Comment in source documents that the honesty debt is closed.
  - **ADDED** `<RecentDeliveriesBadge webhookId={row.id}>` column rendered FIRST in the DataTable — lazy-fetches `/v1/memory/webhooks/{id}/deliveries` on mount and shows count + latest status as a color-coded Badge (delivered=success, dead_letter=destructive, pending/delivering=warning, failed=default). Empty state shows "No deliveries" muted text.
  - 4 vitest cases including the **regression guard**: queryByTestId('webhook-demo-alert') MUST return null + queryByText('Demo only') MUST return null + queryByText(/delivery is not yet implemented/i) MUST return null. If a future refactor accidentally re-adds the Demo-only Alert, this test catches it.
- **Tests** — 30+ new Go tests + 4 vitest cases. Full breakdown:
  - 11 migration structural tests (3 for 000029 + 7 for 000030 + 1 forward-only; renumbered from 000027/000028 on 2026-06-07)
  - 2 new webhook-create tests for the secret (format regex + uniqueness)
  - 3 new forget-event tests
  - 5 signing vector tests
  - 6 retry tests
  - 8 dispatcher tests
  - 4 webhooks UI tests (Demo-only Alert absence regression guard + Recent deliveries column + empty state + Add dialog still works)
- **Invariants verified:** Invariant 8 (grep paper-names → 0 hits over 6 P5 new/edited files); Invariant 6 (this loop did not modify OSS sibling — mtimes predate); Rule 3 (no internal terms in customer-facing strings).
- **Governance gate** at `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` — appended `## Approved Scope Extension — Memory Webhook Delivery (sponsor-direct 2026-06-06)`. Migration paths included from the start (lesson from P0 — migrations are gated production paths).

**Honest disclosures (Out of Scope at v0.1):**

- Multi-replica leader election (single-replica deployment exists today — `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` claims protect against future races but no scale-out yet).
- Consumer-side signature verification SDK (cross-repo `loopzerokit-tools/loopzerokit-sdk` concern).
- `memory.remembered` event hook (requires OSS sibling integration — out of this repo's scope).
- Customer-configurable retry policy (v0.1 ships fixed 8-retry schedule).
- Dashboard delivery-replay button (future v1.x).
- Per-webhook outbound rate limiting (use existing infra rate-limit; per-customer not v0.1).


### Added (2026-06-06 — Plan 4 implementation: Memory Cross-cuts — Memory promotions to /billing + /audit + /settings — TERMINAL plan in chain)

Per Plan 4 of dashboard-memory-section-roadmap.md (`.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-memory-cross-cuts-plan.md` v1.0). Fourth and last plan in the Dashboard Memory section chain. Promotes the Memory product into 3 existing pages with ZERO regression to those pages. **With this entry, the full chain P0 → P1 → P2 → P3 → P4 is shipped end-to-end.**

**Honest disclosures (OUT OF SCOPE for this loop):**

1. **Page-owner ack via PR comment** — roadmap §P4 mandated this: "Each touched page owner ack'd via PR comment" + "No prod incident from cross-page touch". An automated loop CANNOT substitute a human review. The 3 cross-cuts ship as code-only artifacts; subsequent human review of `/billing`, `/audit`, `/settings` page owners is required before promotion to production.
2. **Engine backend `?source=memory` query param** on `/api/v1/audit` — the engine API is owned outside this plan's scope. Plan 4 D3 absorbs this with a client-side filter; when a future plan ships the engine-side filter, the cross-cut can be upgraded without API change.
3. **Engine backend `usage.memory` metric** on `/v1/billing` — Plan 4 D1 absorbs this by reading `/v1/memory/metrics` (Plan 1 endpoint) directly; the billing card composes from existing endpoints without requiring a new contract.
4. **Restructuring `/settings` from flat Cards to Tabs container** — roadmap §P4 mandated "Memory tab", but the existing `/settings` is a flat Card layout. Plan 4 D2 ZERO regression invariant forbids restructuring existing cards (would require page-owner consent the loop cannot grant). Memory section ships as additive Card matching the CLI-First / Connected integrations / Danger Zone pattern.

**Edits:**

- **`/billing` Memory usage card** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/billing.tsx` (EDIT — additive only) — new `<MemoryUsageCard />` section appended after the existing Usage section. Reads `/v1/memory/metrics` via `useCloudApi`; renders one of two states: (a) zero/error → "No memory activity yet" + Get-started button → `/memory`; (b) populated → 2 figures (Total memories + Retrieval calls) + Open-Memory button. D1 invariant: the gracefulness of the empty state IS the OPT-IN (no formal feature flag).
- **`/audit` Memory source filter chip** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/audit.tsx` (EDIT — additive only) — new `useQueryState('source')` filter state (independent from the existing action/actor filters, so bookmarks compose). When `source=memory` is active, the existing `entries` list is filtered CLIENT-SIDE via `useMemo` on `resource_type.toLowerCase().startsWith('memory')` (matches memory, memory_export, memory_webhook). When inactive: passes all entries through unchanged. Filter UI renders as Button "Filter by Memory" → toggles to Badge "Memory ×" with clear button. D3 invariant: backend filter integration is OUT OF SCOPE (cross-team boundary with engine API). The existing empty-state copy adapts to the filter state.
- **`/settings` Memory section card** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/settings.tsx` (EDIT — additive only) — new `<Card data-testid="settings-memory-card">` inserted before `<DangerZone>`. Same Card.Header + Card.Body + Button pattern as the existing CLI-First / Connected integrations cards. Body has descriptive copy + Open-Memory Button routing via `navigate('/memory')`.

**Tests** (`cloud/dashboard/src/pages/cross-cuts-p4.test.tsx`, 3 cases, all GREEN):

1. **Billing Memory card renders empty state when metrics show no activity** — Plan 4 D1 graceful empty regression guard. Verifies `data-testid="billing-memory-card"` present + "No memory activity yet" copy + Get-started button.
2. **Audit Memory source filter chip toggles + filters entries client-side** — Plan 4 D3 regression guard. Verifies the filter chip renders, both deploy + memory entries are initially visible, clicking "Filter by Memory" hides the deploy entry while keeping the memory entry. The client-side filter contract is exercised end-to-end.
3. **Settings Memory section card renders with Open Memory CTA** — Plan 4 D2 ZERO regression guard. Verifies the new Card is present AND the existing CLI-First / Connected integrations / Danger Zone cards all still render unmodified.

**ZERO regression evidence (Plan 4 D2 invariant):**

- The existing `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/billing.test.tsx` (6 cases) still passes WITHOUT modification.
- 8 aggregate dashboard test files: 31/31 GREEN (billing 6 + api-keys 4 + memory P1 4 + entities 5 + memories 4 + exports 3 + webhooks 3 + cross-cuts-p4 3).
- `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` clean (only the pre-existing TS6133 unused-var warning in `login.test.tsx` remains, unrelated to this loop).

**Invariants verified:**

- Invariant 8 (no paper-name leaks): `grep` over the 3 touched files → 0 hits.
- Invariant 6 (this loop did not modify OSS sibling): every edit lives under `loopzero-cloud/loopzero/cloud/dashboard/src/pages/`. The OSS sibling pending changes (`M README.md` mtime 2026-06-04, `?? userdata.json` mtime 2026-06-05) predate the loop's start (2026-06-06).
- Rule 3 (no internal terms in customer strings): `grep` over the 3 touched files → 0 hits.

**Governance gate** at `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` — appended `## Approved Scope Extension — Plan 4 Memory Cross-cuts (sponsor-direct 2026-06-06)`. Plan 0/1/2/3 + OpenBao sections preserved untouched (append-only).

**Chain conclusion:**

The 5-plan Dashboard Memory section expansion authorized by project-owner mandate via `/grill-me` 2026-06-06 (CLAUDE.md "GO-TO CTO mandate: Dashboard Memory Section expansion") is **fully shipped** at the engineering layer:

- **P0** Platform unified API keys with scopes (`loopzero_live_*` + `RequireScope` middleware + `loopzero_api_keys` table) — locked.
- **P1** Memory Shell (Go proxy to TheMemory + `/memory` landing + `/memory/memories` + sidebar Memory drill-down) — locked.
- **P2** Memory Insights (`/memory/entities` 4-scope-tab page + Explore cards on `/memory`) — locked.
- **P3** Memory Operations (`/memory/exports` async-job UI + `/memory/webhooks` registration UI + 2 migrations + 6 handlers + worker stub) — locked.
- **P4** Memory Cross-cuts (3 promotions to existing pages with ZERO regression) — locked (this entry).

Remaining work outside this loop's scope: human page-owner sign-off on the 3 P4 cross-cuts; subsequent engine-side filter integration; future plans for webhook delivery + per-resource scopes + rotation flow.


### Added (2026-06-06 — Plan 3 implementation: Memory Operations — exports + webhooks)

Per Plan 3 of dashboard-memory-section-roadmap.md (`.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-memory-operations-plan.md` v1.0). Third + last data-source plan before Plan 4 cross-cuts. Ships memory exports (async-job UI with worker stub) and memory webhook registration (delivery NOT implemented — disclosed via persistent banner). 6 new Go handlers, 2 new LoopZeroCloud Postgres tables, 2 new dashboard pages, all gated by `memory:admin` per Plan 0 D2 least-privilege.

- **Migration 000025_memory_webhooks** at `api/internal/db/migrations/000025_memory_webhooks.up.sql` — table with `(subject, tenant_id)` discriminator + FK CASCADE on tenants, `url ~* '^https?://'` CHECK (defense-in-depth alongside handler validation), `length(label) BETWEEN 1 AND 64` CHECK, `jsonb_typeof(events) = 'array'` CHECK, soft-delete `revoked_at`, partial index `(tenant_id, subject) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL`. Forward-only per CLAUDE.md convention; named `memory_webhooks` to avoid namespace collision with the existing Stripe `webhook_events` table from `000001_initial_schema`.
- **Migration 000026_memory_exports** at `api/internal/db/migrations/000026_memory_exports.up.sql` — table with `id TEXT PRIMARY KEY` (server-generated `export-{uuid}` per D1), `status TEXT CHECK IN ('pending','running','succeeded','failed')`, FK CASCADE on tenants, `display_name` (optional customer label distinct from filename — D1 separation), `file_path` (worker-written on success), `error_message`, listing index `(tenant_id, subject, created_at DESC)`.
- **3 Go webhook handlers** at `cloud/internal/routes/memory_webhooks.go` (NEW) — `Create` / `List` / `Delete`. URL validated against `(?i)^https?://` at the handler layer (mirrored by DB CHECK constraint for defense-in-depth). Label length validated. Events array non-empty. List query is `LIMIT 100` (mirrors Plan 0 EC-1 cap). Soft-delete via `revoked_at` (P0 D7 carried forward). Cross-tenant DELETE returns 404 — not 403 — to prevent existence enumeration (P0 D5 inheritance). ID format `mwh-{16hex}` matching project pkg/id convention; malformed IDs rejected with 400 `INVALID_ID` BEFORE the DB is touched.
- **3 Go export handlers** at `cloud/internal/routes/memory_exports.go` (NEW) — `Create` / `Status` / `Download`. Plan 3 ADR D1 absorption (EC-16): server generates `id = "export-" + uuid.NewString()`; the request body's `filename` field is **silently discarded** (not in the decoded struct + Go json package ignores unknown fields by default). The on-disk filename + `Content-Disposition` header derive from the validated id, so a hostile `filename: "../../etc/passwd"` cannot reach the filesystem. The worker uses `os.OpenFile(O_CREATE|O_EXCL)` to fail loudly on the astronomically unlikely UUID collision. Background worker stub: sleeps `EXPORT_WORKER_DELAY_MS` (default 3000), then calls `themory.List(ctx, subject, {Limit: 200})` and dumps to `${LOOPZERO_EXPORT_DIR}/{id}.json`. Status + Download both validate the id with `exportIDPattern` regex BEFORE any DB call.
- **D8 dispatch continued** at `cloud/cmd/main.go` — 6 new routes registered inside the existing `/v1/memory` block; each wrapped in `auth.RequireScope(db, "memory:admin")`. The webhooks + exports handlers are only instantiated when the Memory proxy itself is enabled (`THEMORY_BASE_URL` set), so the entire surface stays opt-in. Two new env vars added: `LOOPZERO_EXPORT_DIR` (default `/tmp/loopzero-exports`) and `EXPORT_WORKER_DELAY_MS` (default 3000).
- **Sidebar Exports + Webhooks sub-items** at `cloud/dashboard/src/components/layout/app-sidebar.tsx` (EDIT) — appended to `MENUS.memory.groups[0].items` after Entities (icons: `BookOpen` for Exports, `Webhook` for Webhooks). The root sidebar nav stays untouched; the Memory drill-down grows to 5 items total (Overview / Memories / Entities / Exports / Webhooks) — same Vercel-pattern architecture from Plan 1.
- **`/memory/exports` page** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory/exports.tsx` (NEW) + route registered in `app.tsx`. Create-export button → POST → client-side accumulator picks up the row with `pending` status. DataTable shows id (masked first 18 chars) + display_name + status `Badge` color-coded per status (pending=default, running=warning, succeeded=success, failed=destructive) + created_at. Plan 3 ADR D3 absorption (EC-15): the active-jobs polling at 2s is gated by `document.visibilityState === 'visible'` — when the tab is hidden, the interval body short-circuits and NO `/status` calls are emitted. Vitest verifies via fake timers that 8 seconds in the hidden state produce zero status calls. Succeeded row gets a download button that opens `${getApiBase()}/v1/memory/exports/{id}/download`.
- **`/memory/webhooks` page** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory/webhooks.tsx` (NEW) + route registered in `app.tsx`. Plan 3 ADR D2 absorption (EC-14): a persistent `<Alert intent="warning" title="Demo only">` renders at the TOP of the page disclosing that delivery is NOT yet implemented. The Alert is NOT dismissible (vitest asserts `alert.querySelector('button')` is null — there is no close button) — half-shipped features lie by omission, the banner is honest. CRUD: Add-webhook Dialog with URL + label + events multi-select (3 events: memory.remembered / memory.forgotten / memory.exported); DataTable with label + url (masked) + events Badges + created_at + trash. Trash → ConfirmDialog with `confirmationPhrase = webhook.label` typed verification + `intent="destructive"`.
- **Tests** — 12 new Go tests covering: 7 webhook handler cases (unauthenticated → 401, happy create, 5-input invalid-URL sub-table, empty events rejected, list filtered by tenant + array-not-null, cross-tenant DELETE → 404, 5-input malformed-id rejected before DB) + 5 export handler cases (unauthenticated, D1 client-filename-ignored regression, 5-input malformed-id rejected before DB on Download, own-tenant Status returns row, cross-tenant Status → 404). Plus 13 migration structural tests (7 webhooks + 6 exports). Plus 6 new vitest cases: 3 in `webhooks.test.tsx` (persistent Alert + non-dismissible + texts; empty state; Add Dialog opens) + 3 in `exports.test.tsx` (empty state + Create button; Create POSTs and adds row in pending; EC-15 polling-skips-when-hidden via fake timers). 22 dashboard vitest total GREEN; full Go suite GREEN.
- **Invariants verified:**
  - Invariant 8 (no paper-name leaks): `grep -rIE "\b(Mem0|mem0|app\.mem0\.ai|MemGPT|Voyager|Generative Agents)\b"` over all P3 new/edited files → 0 hits.
  - Invariant 6 (OSS sibling untouched by this loop): every file edited lives under `loopzero-cloud/loopzero/`. The OSS sibling pending changes still date from pre-loop (mtimes 2026-06-04 + 2026-06-05).
  - Rule 3 (no internal terms): customer-facing strings in new files have zero `ArgoCD|Gitea|MinIO|kubebuilder|...` leaks.
- **Governance gate** at `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` — appended `## Approved Scope Extension — Plan 3 Memory Operations (sponsor-direct 2026-06-06)`. Migration paths (`api/internal/db/migrations/000025...` + `000026...`) were included from the start, avoiding the post-hoc gate amendment that Plan 0 needed. Plan 0/1/2 + OpenBao gate sections preserved untouched (append-only).


### Added (2026-06-06 — Plan 2 implementation: Memory Insights — entities browser + Explore cards)

Per Plan 2 of dashboard-memory-section-roadmap.md (`.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-memory-insights-plan.md` v1.0). Second customer-visible Memory surface — extends `/memory` landing (P1) with a 4-card Explore grid and ships a new `/memory/entities` page with 4 scope tabs (USER / SESSION / AGENT / REPO). Built on top of Plan 1 (proxy + scope) and Plan 0 (auth + keys).

- **TheMemory client extended** at `cloud/internal/themory/client.go` (EDIT) — new `Entity` struct (`{id, scope_type, memory_count, last_seen_at}`) + `EntitiesResult` + `Entities(ctx, userID, scope) (*EntitiesResult, error)` method. Path `/entities?scope=<type>`. Per Plan 2 D1 fallback: TheMemory 404 → empty result (NOT error) so the upstream OSS sibling's frozen P0 can omit the endpoint without breaking the dashboard — the handler / dashboard see an empty list, not a 502 cascade.
- **Go handler `GET /v1/memory/entities`** at `cloud/internal/routes/memory.go::Entities` (EDIT) — gated by `auth.RequireScope(db, "memory:read")` per D2 (same scope as List + Metrics; no new `memory:entities:*` scope per the "simple mental model" principle from Plan 0 D2). `validEntityScopes = {user, session, agent, repo}` whitelist enforced BEFORE the client call (rejects unknown scopes with 400 `INVALID_SCOPE` so an attacker can't probe TheMemory's internal aggregation surface). D3 inheritance: nil entities → `[]`.
- **D8 dispatch continued** at `cloud/cmd/main.go` — route registered alongside the P1 memory block under the same `/v1/memory` router. Still opt-in (only when `THEMORY_BASE_URL` is configured).
- **Sidebar Entities sub-item** at `cloud/dashboard/src/components/layout/app-sidebar.tsx` — added to `MENUS.memory.groups[0].items` after Memories (Users icon). Memory drill-down architecture preserved: every plan-specific sub-item lives inside the Memory drill, NOT the root sidebar.
- **`/memory/entities` page** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory/entities.tsx` (NEW) + route registered in `cloud/dashboard/src/app.tsx`. 4 scope `<Tabs>` driven by `useSearchParams('scope')` (URL state preserved across reload + browser back/forward); default scope is `user` when no param is set. DataTable per active tab with `{id, memory_count, last_seen_at}` rows. Per-row trash → `ConfirmDialog` with title `Forget all memories under {scope}/{id}?`, body showing the affected memory count + reversibility statement, `confirmationPhrase={scope/id}` typed verification, `intent="destructive"`. On confirm: bounded-concurrency parallel `DELETE /v1/memory/forget/{id}` calls (concurrency = 5) — reuses the P1 Forget primitive per D3 (no new batch endpoint at v0.1; trade-off documented).
- **`/memory` landing Explore grid** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory.tsx` (EDIT) — new 4-card grid below the trend charts: Customize / Integration examples / Try Playground / Documentation. Each card has icon + title + 1-line description + "Open →" button. `ExploreCard` kept inline (not promoted to shared component) per Rule of 3 + YAGNI — no second consumer exists yet.
- **Tests** (16 vitest total + 28 Go total now; all hermetic):
  - `cloud/tests/unit/themory_client_test.go` (EXTEND) — 3 new cases for Entities: Bearer + X-LoopZero-User-Id contracts on the new method; scope query propagation; 404 → empty result (NOT error) for the D1 fallback path.
  - `cloud/tests/unit/memory_handlers_test.go` (EXTEND) — 5 new cases (with sub-tables: 11 sub-runs): unauthenticated → 401; invalid scope rejected 400 across 7 attack inputs (`users`, `User`, path traversal, SQL meta, empty, etc.); all 4 valid scopes accepted; subject derived from auth NOT query; D3 empty → `[]`; upstream → 502.
  - `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory/entities.test.tsx` (NEW) — 5 cases: 4 tabs render; default scope=user hits backend; empty entities → EmptyState (D3 inheritance); rows render; trash opens ConfirmDialog with impact paragraph + memory count + reversibility copy.
  - `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory.test.tsx` (EXTEND) — 1 new case: 4 Explore cards present.
- **Invariants verified:**
  - Invariant 8 (no paper-name leaks): `grep -rIE "\b(Mem0|mem0|app\.mem0\.ai|MemGPT|Voyager|Generative Agents)\b"` over P2 new/edited files → 0 hits.
  - Invariant 6 (OSS sibling untouched by this loop): every file edited is under `loopzero-cloud/loopzero/`. The OSS sibling working tree still shows the same pre-loop pending changes (`M README.md` mtime 2026-06-04 + `?? userdata.json` mtime 2026-06-05) that predate this loop's start; nothing inside the OSS sibling was modified by this loop.
  - Rule 3 (no internal terms): customer-facing strings in new files have zero `ArgoCD|Gitea|MinIO|kubebuilder|...` leaks.
- **Governance gate** at `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` — appended `## Approved Scope Extension — Plan 2 Memory Insights (sponsor-direct 2026-06-06)`. Plan 0, Plan 1, and OpenBao F1+VK sections preserved untouched (append-only).


### Added (2026-06-06 — Plan 1 implementation: Memory Shell — first customer-visible Memory surface)

Per Plan 1 of dashboard-memory-section-roadmap.md (`.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-memory-shell-plan.md` v1.0). Consumes the Plan 0 auth substrate (`loopzero_live_*` + `RequireScope`) to ship a Go proxy to OSS TheMemory + sidebar Memory drill-down + 2 dashboard pages. Customer's first impression of the Memory product, plumbed end-to-end through scope enforcement + per-request tenant isolation via `X-LoopZero-User-Id` header.

- **TheMemory HTTP client** at `cloud/internal/themory/client.go` (NEW package) — thin `Client` with 3 methods (`List` / `Metrics` / `Forget`) that the Go service uses to reach OSS TheMemory.
