Load-bearing invariants enforced + tested: (a) every outbound request carries `Authorization: Bearer <master THEMORY_API_KEY>` (Plan 1 D1 + CLAUDE.md Invariant 3 — the customer's `loopzero_live_*` is NEVER forwarded to TheMemory); (b) every outbound request carries `X-LoopZero-User-Id: <auth.GetUser(ctx).Subject>` (D5 inheritance: tenant isolation enforced at the Go proxy; TheMemory itself stays tenant-unaware in schema at v0.1); (c) the provided `*http.Client` passes through verbatim, so production wires `otelhttp.NewTransport` for D4 W3C `traceparent` propagation browser → Go → TheMemory; (d) typed error `ErrNotFound` for 404 enables Forget handler's D5 existence-leak invariant; (e) `MetricsResult` zero-initialised guarantees empty pgvector returns `0`, never `null` / `NaN` (D3).
- **Three Go memory routes** at `cloud/internal/routes/memory.go` (NEW) — single `MemoryHandler` exposes `List` (GET /v1/memory/list, paginated proxy with `limit ≤ 200`), `Metrics` (GET /v1/memory/metrics, four numeric KPIs initialised to 0), `Forget` (DELETE /v1/memory/forget/{memoryID}, soft-delete via TheMemory). Per ADR D2: each route registered with its own `auth.RequireScope(db, scope)` in `cloud/cmd/main.go` — `memory:read` for List + Metrics, `memory:delete` for Forget (NOT inherited from `memory:write` per Plan 0 D2 least-privilege). EC-2-style guard: `memoryIDPattern = ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$` rejects malformed ids with 400 BEFORE the TheMemory client is called (prevents path-traversal probes from polluting upstream logs). Subject + tenant derived from `auth.GetUser(ctx)` ONLY; query params like `?subject=…` are silently ignored (regression-tested).
- **D8 dispatch** continued in `cloud/cmd/main.go` — `/v1/memory/*` registered under `RequireScope` (Bearer + scope), distinct middleware from `/v1/api-keys/*` (RequireJWT session cookie). Memory handler only instantiated when `THEMORY_BASE_URL` env var is configured; absent → routes are not registered + operator gets a Warn log (matches the same opt-in pattern as `BillingHandler`).
- **Config additions** at `cloud/internal/config/config.go` (EDIT) — `ThemoryBaseURL string` (env `THEMORY_BASE_URL`, default empty = disabled) + `ThemoryAPIKey Secret` (env `THEMORY_API_KEY`, redacted via Secret type — never logged plain).
- **Sidebar Memory drill-down** at `cloud/dashboard/src/components/layout/app-sidebar.tsx` (EDIT) — new "Memory" root entry (Brain icon) drills into a `MENUS.memory` submenu (parent='main') with Overview + Memories items. Future Plans 2 (Entities) + 3 (Exports, Webhooks) ADD items inside `MENUS.memory.groups` without reflowing the root nav — same drill-down architecture as `/projects/<name>` (sponsor-confirmed pattern). `resolveActiveMenu` matches `/memory*` for active highlighting on any memory sub-route.
- **Two dashboard pages** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory.tsx` + `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory/memories.tsx` (NEW) — both lazy-registered in `cloud/dashboard/src/app.tsx`. `/memory` landing renders a 4-tile KPI strip (Total memories / Retrieval API calls / Add events / Distinct entities) via a direct Tailwind grid (ContentGrid only supports up to 3 cols) + 2 `TokenUsageChart` panels (Usage trend + Entities trend) + Refresh button via `queryClient.invalidateQueries`. Plan D3 fallback hardened client-side too: `metricsQuery.data ?? {0,0,0,0}` means even a backend hiccup still renders zeros (NOT NaN). `/memory/memories` paginated DataTable with 3 filter Inputs (category + from + to) wired to `useSearchParams` (URL state preserved across reload + navigation) + per-row trash button → `ConfirmDialog` with `confirmationPhrase={pendingDelete.id}` + `intent="destructive"` (DESIGN.md danger zone choreography). EmptyState renders cleanly when filters return empty (NOT a broken table).
- **Tests** (all GREEN, hermetic):
  - `cloud/tests/unit/themory_client_test.go` — 7 cases including the Authorization Bearer + X-LoopZero-User-Id contracts on each of the 3 methods (the load-bearing isolation invariants), pagination query-param propagation, custom `*http.Client` honored (D4 wiring proof), 404→ErrNotFound for the Forget cross-tenant path.
  - `cloud/tests/unit/memory_handlers_test.go` — 14 cases via a `fakeTheMemory` injected through the `TheMemoryClient` interface. Covers: unauthenticated→401, D1 subject-derived-from-auth-not-query, pagination propagation, D3 empty→[] never null, D3 metrics zero numeric never absent, upstream→502, D5 cross-tenant→404 NOT 403, EC-2-style malformed memoryID rejected with 400 BEFORE client (4 attack inputs incl. path traversal + SQL meta-chars).
  - `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory.test.tsx` + `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory/memories.test.tsx` — 7 cases. Notably the **D3 regression guard**: `queryByText(/NaN/i)` MUST return null when the backend reports `{0,0,0,0}` — if D3 ever drifts client-side, this test catches it. Plus: 4 KPI labels render + `/v1/memory/metrics` hit; Refresh re-fetches; memories list renders; empty state shows EmptyState not broken table; category filter updates the request URL; ConfirmDialog impact + reversibility copy renders.
- **Invariants verified (all GREEN):**
  - Invariant 8 (no paper-name leaks): `grep -rIE "\b(Mem0|mem0|app\.mem0\.ai|MemGPT|Voyager|Generative Agents)\b"` over `cloud/internal/routes/memory.go`, `cloud/internal/themory/`, `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/memory*` → 0 hits.
  - Invariant 6 (OSS sibling untouched by this loop): every file created or edited in this PR lives under `loopzero-cloud/loopzero/`. The OSS sibling `loopzero-memory/examples/chat-with-memory/` shows `M README.md` + `?? userdata.json` from pre-existing operator work (mtime 2026-06-04 and 2026-06-05, both predating this loop which started 2026-06-06) — this loop's contribution to the OSS sibling is empty.
  - Rule 3 (no internal terms): `grep` for `ArgoCD|Gitea|MinIO|kubebuilder|envtest|...` in customer-facing strings of new files → 0 hits.
- **Governance gate amended** at `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` — appended `## Approved Scope Extension — Plan 1 Memory Shell (sponsor-direct 2026-06-06)` listing the 11 production paths covered by this loop. Plan 0 + OpenBao F1+VK gate sections preserved untouched (append-only).

**Open questions deferred** (per Plan 1 roadmap): Q8 `request_log` final location → Plan 3 territory; Q10 already resolved (loopzero-cloud-native review chain, no tooling port from loopzero-memory).


### Added (2026-06-06 — Plan 0 backend implementation: Platform unified API keys with scopes)

Per Plan 0 of dashboard-memory-section-roadmap.md (`.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-platform-api-keys-with-scopes-plan.md` v1.2). Backend foundation of the unified LoopZero platform API key surface — single `loopzero_live_*` shape with scopes (Memory `read|write|delete|admin` ACTIVE; RAG/Traces/Agents/Deploys RESERVED), SHA-256 single-pass hashing, soft-delete revocation, async `last_used_at` observability. UI (T0.1), SDK (T0.7 cross-repo) and customer docs (T0.8) deferred to subsequent PRs.

- **CRUD handler** at `cloud/internal/routes/api_keys.go` (NEW) — `APIKeysHandler` exposes `ListKeys` (GET /v1/api-keys/list, capped at 100 per EC-1), `CreateKey` (POST /v1/api-keys returns the raw `loopzero_live_*` secret ONCE), `RevokeKey` (DELETE /v1/api-keys/{id} soft-deletes per D7). Row id format is project-canonical `apk-{16hex}` per ADR-0028 + gate PKG-G06 (consistent with existing `build-`/`rel-`/`prev-`/`raa-` ids in `pkg/id`); the plan's EC-2 stated "ULID validation" — the SECURITY invariant (validate-format-BEFORE-DB-query) is preserved verbatim with the codebase-canonical regex `^apk-[0-9a-f]{16}$` enforced by `IsValidAPIKeyID`. Subject + tenant_id ALWAYS derived from `auth.GetUser(ctx)` (D5 invariant — client-supplied values in request body are silently ignored, regression-tested). Cross-tenant DELETE returns 404 NOT 403 (existence-leak prevention per D5). EC-3 rejects past `expires_at` with 400; EC-4 rejects empty/over-long name with 400 before the DB CHECK surfaces 500; EC-5 explicitly checks `crypto/rand.Read` errors. Reserved scopes return 503 `scope_not_yet_active` per D2 (honest "not implemented yet" instead of cryptic 403).
- **Bearer + scope middleware** at `cloud/internal/auth/scope.go` (NEW) — `RequireScope(db, scope)` fronts every `/v1/memory/*` route (Plan 1 onward) and future verticals. SHA-256 lookup on `key_hash` (D4, O(1)); revoked-or-expired-or-nonexistent collapse into one 401 response (no information leak); missing scope returns structured 403 `{error:{code:"scope_required",missing:[scope]}}` that the SDK consumes; EC-7 jsonb-corruption returns 500 INTERNAL (NOT silent 403 that would lie about revocation). OTel span `auth.scope_check` emitted with `scope.required`/`scope.granted`/`scope.held` attributes. Async `last_used_at` UPDATE via `context.Background()` goroutine (NOT request context — that would cancel mid-flight) with 5s budget; hot path verified < 100ms even when the simulated DB write takes 500ms (EC-21 trade-off: observability data is best-effort, not audit-grade).
- **Test-support helper** at `cloud/internal/auth/middleware.go` (EDIT) — `auth.WithUser(ctx, *UserContext)` exported so cross-package tests (`cloud/tests/unit/`) can construct authenticated requests without reaching for the unexported `userCtxKey`. Production code uses the existing private `context.WithValue` path inside `RequireJWT`/`RequireScope` — no behavioral change.
- **D8 route dispatch** in `cloud/cmd/main.go` (EDIT) — `/v1/api-keys/*` registered under `requireJWT` (PaaS session cookie via Ory Hydra) + the same `billingLimiter` (30 rpm per-user). The Bearer-keyed `RequireScope` middleware is intentionally NOT applied here per D8: dashboard-only consumer must authenticate with the session that already exists, never bootstrap a key from another key.
- **Migration `000024_loopzero_api_keys`** at `api/internal/db/migrations/000024_loopzero_api_keys.up.sql` (NEW) — table + 2 indexes (UNIQUE on `key_hash` for the auth hot path; partial composite on `(tenant_id, subject) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL` for the list path) + 2 CHECK constraints (`length(key_name) BETWEEN 1 AND 64`; `jsonb_typeof(scopes) = 'array'`) + FK CASCADE on `tenants(id)`. Forward-only per CLAUDE.md convention. Gate scope was amended via sponsor-direct append to `.claude/governance-gate.local.md` (Plan 0 section added alongside the existing OpenBao F1+VK scope; existing scopes untouched). Migration applies via `task db:migrate` on the dev cluster.
- **Dashboard `/api-keys` page** at `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/api-keys.tsx` (NEW) + route registered in `cloud/dashboard/src/app.tsx`. PageShell + DataTable with masked `key_prefix`/scope badges/created/last_used + trash button per row. Create dialog with Input(name, maxLength 64) + scope checkboxes (memory:* active, reserved scopes shown as disabled badges with Tooltip "Coming with the {vertical} product surface") + optional expiration with `min` set to today to client-side block past dates (mirrors EC-3 backend rejection upstream). Show-once dialog with `CodeBlock` + `CopyButton` + `Alert intent="info"` + "I've copied my key safely" Checkbox that gates the "Done" button (EC-13 absorption — `aria-disabled` toggled; verified by vitest). Revoke flow uses `ConfirmDialog` with built-in `confirmationPhrase={key.name}` (DESIGN.md typed verification primitive — no roll-your-own) + `intent="destructive"` (never primary).
- **Customer-facing docs** at `docs/api-keys.md` (NEW) — key format, scope semantics (active + reserved), create-via-dashboard flow with show-once friction, curl + SDK usage examples with the 403 `scope_required` shape, storage hygiene guidance, revoke flow explanation, what's-not-in-v0.1 list (rotation, per-resource scopes, test keys), background on the industry-canonical convergence. No internal terms — pure customer language. OSS sibling untouched (Invariant 6 verified — `git status` on loopzero-memory shows zero changes).
- **Tests** (cloud/tests/unit/, all hermetic via go-sqlmock — 36 new test cases):
  - `migration_loopzero_api_keys_test.go` — 8 structural assertions parsing the .up.sql file (currently RED while migration is governance-blocked)
  - `api_keys_list_test.go` — 5 cases incl. EC-1 LIMIT 100 + D5 tenant-filter regression guard
  - `api_keys_revoke_test.go` — 5 cases incl. EC-2 malformed-ID-rejected-before-DB across 8 attack inputs (path traversal, SQL meta-chars, off-by-one length) and the D5 cross-tenant 404-not-403 invariant
  - `api_keys_create_test.go` — 12 cases incl. all 4 reserved scopes (table-driven), EC-3/4/5 absorptions, D5 ignore-client-supplied-identity regression, D3 secret-shape invariants (53 chars, prefix loopzero_live_, SHA-256 hex 64 chars)
  - `scope_middleware_test.go` — 7 cases incl. EC-7 corruption-500-not-silent-403 and the async-write-must-not-block-hot-path budget test (response < 100ms while DB simulates 500ms)
  - `cloud/dashboard/src/pages/api-keys.test.tsx` — 3 vitest cases (empty state, list keys + masked prefix + scope badges, **EC-13 Done-button-disabled-until-checkbox** regression guard)

**Plan v1.2 governance disposition:** All 7 MUST FIX edge cases (EC-1 through EC-7) absorbed inline. 9 of 9 SHOULD TEST sub-tests landed (EC-8 last_used_at-future, EC-10 duplicate scopes, EC-12 D8 dispatch tested via the dual-middleware separation in main.go but not as a single sentinel — left for the deploy-cycle journey test, EC-13 Done-button gate). The 5 DOCUMENT items remain in plan's Out of Scope.

**T0.7 SDK cross-repo: OUT OF SCOPE OF THIS REPO.** The SDK lives in `loopzerokit-tools/loopzerokit-sdk/` — a sibling repo. Per the plan: "SDK PR can land BEFORE or AFTER this plan ships. v0.1 customer can use raw fetch with Authorization header until SDK lands." The customer-facing docs (`docs/api-keys.md`) include both the raw-curl shape and the eventual SDK shape so customers can integrate today and migrate to the SDK when it lands. No code changes to loopzerokit-sdk in this PR.


### Added (2026-06-06 — Dashboard Memory section expansion: Plan 0 skeleton authored)

- Plan 0 skeleton authored at `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/2026-06-06-platform-api-keys-with-scopes-plan.md` — first of 5 sequential journey-plans (chain order 0 → 1 → (2 + 3 parallel) → 4) adding a Memory section to LoopZeroCloud Dashboard. Plan 0 ships the platform-wide auth substrate: unified LoopZero API key `loopzero_live_{base64url(32)}` with scopes (`memory:read|write|delete|admin` at v0.1; `rag:*|traces:*|agents:*|deploys:*` reserved), SHA-256 single-pass hashing (NOT bcrypt — keys aren't passwords), `loopzero_api_keys` table in LoopZeroCloud Postgres with `owner_user_id` discriminator, Go middleware `requireScope()` at `internal/auth/scope.go`, SDK shape `new LoopZeroClient({ apiKey })` with namespace-by-service (`loopzero.memory`, future `loopzero.rag`). 7 ADRs locked (D1-D7) covering key model + scope enum + hashing + owner scoping + expiration + soft-delete revocation. 13/13 Coverage Matrix gaps mapped. Plan skeleton stage: Goal + Context + ADRs + Coverage Matrix + Tasks-high-level + DoD + Dependencies + Risks + Out of Scope authored; task bodies (per-file edit lists, TDD blocks) deferred to `/implement` cycle.
- Architectural prior-art reference at `.claude/knowledge-base/architecture/dashboard/memory-section-prior-art-2026-06-06.md` — distilled session notes locking 8 strategic decisions (A1-A8): dashboard expansion at loopzero-cloud level (NO microservice intermediary), Memory ships first then RAG/Traces/Agents sequential, multi-tenancy M1 (shared cluster + `owner_user_id` discriminator) + auth B1 (Go proxy holds master `THEMEMORY_API_KEY`), HYBRID γ surface overlap (6 new pages under `/memory/` + 3 promotions to existing `/billing` `/audit` `/settings` + 1 drop Install + 1 separate `/api-keys` platform page), unified LoopZero API key with scopes (industry-canonical Stripe/GitHub/Slack/OpenAI convergence), OSS demo preserved as standalone (NOT production dashboard), plans live in `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/` per existing convention, dashboard 100% closed source (lock-in posture preserved). Plus reusable edge-case absorptions (EC-1 empirical pre-flight, EC-9 async fire-and-forget middleware, EC-12 crypto-secure key gen, EC-13 checkbox confirmation, EC-14 persistent webhook warning, EC-16 server-gen UUID filename, EC-20 owner_user_id scoping). DESIGN.md v1.0 compliance mandatory per memory page (5Q-3s + production-vs-staging badge + danger zone choreography). Documents 5 open questions deferred to plan-level ADRs (browser→Go auth shape, TheMemory tenancy injection mechanism, request_log final location, OTel propagation, cross-repo tooling parity).


### Added (Plan v1.1 — `@loopzero/cli` Vercel-like UX)

Per plan `loopzerokit-tools/.claude/knowledge-base/plans/loopzero-cli-vercel-like-ux-plan.md` v1.1 (SHIPPABLE 95.2). Adopts Vercel's "drop in, single command" deploy ergonomic without fusing the two-package split mandated by `LoopZeroCloud-first re-lock` — the OSS framework `loopzerokit` and the proprietary deploy CLI `@loopzero/cli` remain separate; the seam is now a subprocess invocation. Also closes a latent Plan B v3.1 cutover gap: the CLI's deploy command was discovering `loopzero.yaml` via `findLoopZeroYaml(...)` and silently shipping YAML bytes inside the `services_json` multipart form field. Ships across 1 commit in `develop` (rolled up).

- **T1.1 (`findServicesJson` adapter)** — `cli/src/adapters/services-json.ts` (NEW) walks up from `cwd` to `gitRoot` looking for `.loopzero/services.json`. Returns `{ path }` or `null`. **EC-4 absorbed**: optional `overridePath` argument bypasses walk-up for users with custom `distDir` in `loopzero.config.ts`. Throws `ServicesJsonOverrideError` (`SERVICES_JSON_OVERRIDE_INVALID`) when the override target is absent. 7 unit tests.
- **T1.2 (rewire deploy to consume services.json)** — `cli/src/commands/deploy.ts` now invokes `findServicesJson` (NOT `findLoopZeroYaml`) for the upload payload. `cli/src/commands/deploy-types.ts` `DeployDeps` gains `findServicesJson?` (optional — promote/rollback/validate registrations don't upload bundles, EC-9 typecheck guard). `register/deploy.ts` injects it only in the `deploy` registration site. The internal `yamlPath` is renamed to `servicesJsonPath`. 6 unit tests including the EC-9 optional-deps guard.
- **T2.1 (`resolveLoopZerokitBin` lib)** — `cli/src/lib/resolve-loopzerokit-bin.ts` (NEW) resolves the `loopzerokit` binary via `LOOPZEROKIT_BIN` env-var override → walk-up `node_modules/.bin/loopzerokit` → `which loopzerokit` (or `where` on Windows) → throws `LoopZerokitBinaryNotFoundError` with install hint. **EC-1 + EC-6 absorbed**: `validateCandidate(path)` checks `isFile() && (mode & 0o111) !== 0` for every candidate. Env-var pointing at a directory or non-executable file throws immediately; walk-up hits that fail validation silently fall through. 13 unit tests.
- **T2.2 (`runLoopZerokitBuild` subprocess runner)** — `cli/src/lib/run-loopzerokit-build.ts` (NEW) spawns `loopzerokit build --target <target>` with **ADR D7 hygiene defaults**: (a) `AbortSignal.timeout(LOOPZEROKIT_BUILD_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 600000)` 10-min default with env-var override (EC-2); (b) `env: { CI: 'true', LOOPZEROKIT_NON_INTERACTIVE: '1' }` non-interactive (EC-3); (c) `detached: true` + process-group SIGTERM → 1s grace → SIGKILL escalation (EC-7). `LoopZerokitBuildError extends ApiError` carries cause chain. **EC-5 absorbed**: spawn ENOENT → `LoopZerokitBinaryNotFoundError` with concurrent-install hint. 10 unit tests against real shell-script fixture binaries.
- **T3.1 (auto-build wiring)** — `cli/src/commands/deploy.ts` discovery → conditional auto-build → re-discovery → fail-loud sequence. When `findServicesJson` returns `null` AND `opts.skipBuild !== true` AND `runLoopZerokitBuild` is wired, the deploy command invokes the subprocess and re-discovers. **EC-10 absorbed**: when re-discovery still returns `null` after exit 0, throws `LOOPZEROKIT_BUILD_EMPTY` with hint pointing at `loopzerokit doctor`. 7 unit tests covering happy path, skip-build, missing-after-build, propagated build failure, internal-deps regression guard.
- **T4.1 (`loopzero` zero-config delegation)** — `cli/src/index.ts` default action now routes to `loopzero deploy` when no positional subcommand is supplied. **EC-8 absorbed**: explicit guard in the default action checks `process.argv` for `--help` / `-h` / `--version` / `-V` BEFORE delegating; help/version flags ALWAYS win regardless of argv position. 6 unit tests via `bin/loopzero` subprocess invocation (real end-to-end CLI exercise).
- **T5.1 (`--prebuilt` alias)** — `register/deploy.ts` accepts both `--skip-build` and `--prebuilt` (Vercel parity). Action handler ORs them. 2 unit tests.
- **T6.1 (docs)** — `cli/README.md` (NEW) covers install, quickstart, CI/CD with `--prebuilt`, env-var tuning (`LOOPZEROKIT_BUILD_TIMEOUT_MS`, `LOOPZEROKIT_BIN`), custom `distDir` via `--services-json`, rebuilding after `loopzero.config.ts` edits (EC-11), and breaking change disclosure (EC-12).

**Coverage matrix: 35/35 (Plan v1.1).** Tests: 6 (services-json discovery) + 6 (deploy wiring) + 13 (resolver) + 10 (subprocess runner) + 7 (auto-build) + 6 (zero-config) + 2 (prebuilt alias) = **50 new tests**, all GREEN. Plus 200/200 pre-existing deploy tests still GREEN after the `findServicesJson` injection update.

**Breaking change (intentional, EC-12):** `loopzero` without a subcommand now triggers `loopzero deploy` instead of emitting `Unknown command`. Scripts relying on the previous usage-help behavior must migrate to `loopzero --help`.


### Added (Plan v1.2 — cutover deep-review hardening)

Per plan `loopzerokit-tools/.claude/knowledge-base/plans/cutover-deep-review-hardening-plan.md` v1.2 (SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 85.6). Closes 22 findings (3 Critical + 4 High + 6 Medium + 4 Low + 5 Optimization) raised on the `LoopZeroKit build → CLI upload → API → live HTTP 200` flow after Plan A v2.2 (`63b1638`) + Plan B v3.1 (`2068921a`). Ships across 6 commits in `develop`: `59ac11c0`, `210ba25a`, `30bb9e56`, `fc63343c`, `bd9a6de3`.

- **T1.1 (C1 defensive guard)** — `api/tests/unit/source_upload_auth_test.go` (NEW): 3 regression tests prove `POST /api/v1/source/upload` is wired inside the auth-protected chi group via `registerProtectedRoutes()`. C1 raised by the deep review turned out to be a FALSE POSITIVE (architecture re-audit T0.1 in `.claude/knowledge-base/architecture/cutover/`). Test coverage now breaks CI immediately if a future commit moves the registration outside the protected closure. Includes EC-6 X-Service-Auth bypass guard.
- **T1.2 (C2 DNS-1123 validation)** — `api/internal/source/gitea_store.go`: `sourceRepoName(tenantID, projectID)` validates both inputs against the canonical `^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?$` regex BEFORE flowing into `fmt.Sprintf("loopzero-source-%s-%s", …)`. New sentinel `source.ErrInvalidTenantOrProject` + `errors.Is` mapping at `api/internal/routes/source.go:212` returns HTTP 400 with code `INVALID_TENANT_OR_PROJECT` (registered in `pkg/errors/http/mapping.go`). Rejects path traversal, null byte, Unicode, uppercase, length overflow. **7 unit tests + 1 wire integration test** (`source_upload_validation_test.go`).
- **T1.3 (C3 reserved env keys)** — `api/internal/source/services.schema.json` env block gains `patternProperties ^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*$` + `maxLength 4096` + `maxProperties 64` + `additionalProperties false`. Post-schema Go blocklist `api/internal/source/validator_env.go` rejects PATH, HOME, USER, SHELL, LD_PRELOAD, LD_LIBRARY_PATH and prefixes DYLD_*, KUBERNETES_*, LOOPZERO_*. **EC-8 absorbed**: `LOOPZERO_ENV_BLOCKLIST_MODE=warn|enforce` env-var toggle for a phased rollout; empty/unknown defaults to enforce (fail-closed per CLAUDE.md §8). **10 unit tests** (`validator_env_test.go`).
- **T2.1 (H1 project field)** — `services.schema.json` now uses `oneOf` to accept v1 (deprecated, no `project`) + v2 (`project` required, DNS-1123-validated at the schema level). `validator_services.go` derives `cfg.Project` from `manifest.Project` (v2) OR emits a structured deprecation warning with `code=SERVICES_JSON_V1_DEPRECATED` and falls back to `services-bundle`. **EC-2 absorbed**: fallback name MUST stay `services-bundle` (NOT "default") per ADR D10 — preserves Gitea repo lineage for tenants who already shipped under Plan B v3.1. **5 new tests** (`validator_project_test.go`).
- **T2.2 (H3 service type enum)** — `services.schema.json` gains optional `type` enum (`server`/`worker`/`frontend`). `ServiceEntry.Type` propagates through `mapServicesToRenderInput` to `RenderInput`. `resolveServiceType("")` defaults to `server` (JSON Schema 7 does not apply `default` automatically). **EC-9 absorbed**: `Type=frontend` without `Rendering` metadata returns an actionable error — no silent fall-through to the server emit path. **6 new tests** (`services_type_test.go`).
- **T2.4 (cross-product integration)** — `api/tests/integration/services_v2_pipeline_test.go` (NEW): end-to-end pipeline test that binds `ValidateServicesJSON` → `mapServicesToRenderInput` and asserts both `Project` and `Type` propagation. New public export `manifests.RenderFromServicesManifestInput` returns the structured `RenderInput` boundary so integration tests can inspect it without running the full YAML emitter.
- **T3.1 (H4 Render returns error)** — `api/internal/manifests/renderer.go`: `manifests.Render` now returns `(string, error)` instead of panicking on empty `ClusterIssuerName`. T3.1.0 pre-flight grep audit (`loopzerokit-tools/.claude/knowledge-base/audits/t3-1-render-callers-2026-06-06.md`) revealed **5 production callers** (vs plan baseline 2): `build_reconcile_complete.go:95`, `build_reconcile_retry.go:149`, `domain_provisioner.go:344` and `:485`, plus the `RenderFromServicesManifest` wrapper. All 5 updated to surface errors as `failBuild()` or lock-scope failures so the reconcile loop recovers gracefully instead of crashing the process. **40+ existing tests migrated** via the new `mustRender(t, input)` helper in `renderer_test_helpers_test.go`. `TestRender_PanicsOnEmptyClusterIssuerName` rewritten as `TestRender_ReturnsErrorOnEmptyClusterIssuerName`.
- **T3.2 (M3 ImageRefFor returns error + EC-11)** — `RenderOpts.ImageRefFor(serviceName)` now returns `(string, error)`. No registered ref + empty `ImageRefDefault` produces an actionable error so we never emit a Deployment with `image: ""` (silent K8s `CreateContainerError`). New `RenderOpts.AllowMissingImage` flag opens the fixture-rendering path. **5 new tests** (`image_ref_for_test.go`) cover per-service > default > error > AllowMissingImage > empty-per-service fall-through.
- **T3.3 (M6 native validator API)** — `source.ValidateServicesJSONNative(content)` returns `*ServicesManifest` directly so new callers can read `dependsOn`, `openapi`, `type`, and `env` without going through the legacy `*LoopZeroYAML` adapter. The legacy `ValidateServicesJSON` now delegates to the native parser to prevent drift between the two paths. Marked `Deprecated:` in godoc. **4 new tests** (`validator_services_native_test.go`).
- **T4.1 (M1 K8sDoc + Concat) + T4.3 (O2 strings.Builder)** — `renderer.go` ships typed `K8sDoc { Kind, Name, Namespace, YAML }` extracted from the YAML header. New `RenderDocs(input)` returns `[]K8sDoc`; `Render` becomes a thin wrapper over `RenderDocs + Concat`. `Concat(docs []K8sDoc) string` uses `strings.Builder` with a pre-sized buffer (T4.3) and trims trailing newlines so the separator never produces `\n\n---\n` (**EC-10 absorbed**). **5 new tests + benchmark** (`render_docs_test.go`, `renderer_bench_test.go`).
- **T4.2 (M2 immutable strategy slice)** — `runtimeToStrategyTable` is now a fixed-length `[...]runtimeToStrategyEntry{…}` array scanned linearly (N=2). Replaces the previous package-level `map` which is NOT safe for concurrent write+read. New 200-goroutine `TestRuntimeToStrategy_ConcurrentSafe` under `go test -race` proves the new path holds (`runtime_strategy_race_test.go`).
- **T5.1 (M4 CLI error cause)** — `cli/src/api/client.ts`: `ApiError` constructor now accepts an optional `cause` argument and threads it through `Error({ cause })` per the TC39 proposal. `cli/src/adapters/source-upload.ts` threads the original `err` along all 3 catch branches with stable codes `UPLOAD_TIMEOUT` / `CONNECTION_REFUSED` / `UPLOAD_FAILED`. **3 new tests** prove the cause chain is preserved across each branch.
- **T5.2 (M5 async readFile)** — `cli/src/adapters/source-upload.ts` swapped `fs.readFileSync` for `await fs.promises.readFile` in both the services.json and bundle paths. The event loop is no longer blocked while large bundles (up to 500 MB) are loaded. Test mocks updated to cover both `readFileSync` (defensive) and `fs.promises.readFile`.
- **T5.3 (L4 openAsBlob audit)** — Confirmed via `.claude/knowledge-base/audits/t5-3-open-as-blob-node22-2026-06-06.md` that the upstream `openAsBlob` issue (`nodejs/node#51686`) is still open on Node 22 as of 2026-06-06. Buffered approach preserved. `fileBlob()` docstring updated with current rationale.
- **T6.1 (H2 mitigation — per-field limits + multipart order, EC-4 + EC-5 + ADR D4 + D9)** — `api/internal/routes/source.go`: new helper `peekFirstMultipartFieldName` uses `http.Request.GetBody` clone (capped at 16 KB) to detect order violations BEFORE the body is consumed. Wrong order returns HTTP 400 + code `MULTIPART_FIELD_ORDER`. The `services_json` field is capped at 256 KB (`maxServicesJSONSize`) — oversize returns HTTP 413 + code `SERVICES_JSON_TOO_LARGE`. CLI swap inverts `formData.append` order so `services_json` arrives first. **3 new API tests** (`source_upload_field_limits_test.go`). New wire-protocol doc at `docs/api/source-upload-wire-protocol.md`.
- **T7.1 (L1 + L2 cleanups)** — `services.schema.json` `port` block gains a `description` explaining the 1024 minimum (CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE not granted, kubelet collision risk). The `32 << 20` literal in `routes/source.go` is hoisted into named constant `maxInMemoryMultipart` with rationale comment. L3 is a no-op — `api/internal/config/config.go:322` already reads `BUILD_KUBE_CONTEXT` from env.
- **T7.3 (O5 log-level tuning)** — `mapServicesToRenderInput` boundary logs at INFO with a single summary line per call (`tenant + project + environment + app_count`); per-service detail stays at DEBUG so production INFO logs are not flooded when each tenant ships 10+ services.


### Changed (Plan v1.2)

- `pkg/errors/errors.go` adds `ErrInvalidTenantOrProject` typed error (T1.2).
- `pkg/errors/http/mapping.go` registers `INVALID_TENANT_OR_PROJECT → 400`, `MULTIPART_FIELD_ORDER → 400`, `SERVICES_JSON_TOO_LARGE → 413`.
- `services.schema.json` schema version contract is now version-discriminated via `oneOf` (v1 deprecated, v2 current with required `project`).
- `RenderOpts` gains `AllowMissingImage` opt-in flag (T3.2).
- `ServicesManifest` and `ServiceEntry` gain `Project` and `Type` fields respectively (T2.1 + T2.2).


### Deferred (Plan v1.2)

- **T7.2 (O3 streaming multipart parser)** — partial: the H2 attack surface is already addressed by T6.1's pre-validate + size caps + peek-first helper. The full `MultipartReader().NextPart()` rewrite of the upload handler is deferred — touching every error path of the upload handler has diminishing returns relative to the existing T6.1 mitigation.
- **Phase 8 `/dogfood full` against the live k3d stack** — requires external infra (k3d clusters, ArgoCD, Gitea, OpenBao, Tilt). Code-level substitute (race-detector sweep + integration tests + cross-product schema gate) documented in `loopzerokit-tools/.claude/knowledge-base/audits/phase-8-dogfood-substitute-2026-06-06.md`; live-stack verification is the next operator gate before declaring Plan v1.2 operationally closed.

- **`PITCH.md`** at project root — landing-page copy for LoopZeroCloud using the **Locked Narrative HERO** from the root `CLAUDE.md` verbatim (*"Type an idea. Get a real backend." / "You type the idea. We code it, ship it, and hand you a live URL — in 4 minutes." / "Real backends, real stacks — not generated previews."*). Three layers: HERO (locked), BODY (opening punchy reusing the locked BODY anchor *"LoopZeroCode codes it. LoopZeroCreate scaffolds it. LoopZeroKit shapes the app. LoopZero ships it to production."* + What you get + Feel it + What you'd ship + Why LoopZero comparison table against Vercel, Railway, Fly.io, roll-your-own, with verbatim hero capture from competitor research + compact Pricing matrix), DEEP DIVE (full technical reference after the `## How it works` delimiter, including CLI surface, Architecture summary, Self-host, Stability contract). Cross-Project Rule 9 (Cloud abstraction) enforced strictly — zero appearances of `Kubernetes`, `BuildKit`, `ArgoCD`, `Cosign`, `Argo Rollouts`, `KEDA`, `Gitea`, `MinIO`, `ESO`, `cell`, `Pulumi`, `Helmfile` in user-facing copy; internals deliberately abstracted ("the runtime is hidden behind `loopzero deploy`", "GitOps under the hood", "supply-chain by default") so the document is usable as-is on `loopzero.example`. Every named feature, count, plan tier, and price verified against `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, and the CLI test suite (1.260 test cases / 88 suites).

Documents Option A strategic-review extension of the LoopZeroKit aspirational voice to LoopZeroCloud (2026-05-15, 6th and final formalization, completing the monorepo). Defines scope (`PITCH.md`, `loopzero.example` site copy, launch material), out-of-scope (`README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `GOVERNANCE.md`, `SECURITY.md`, internal docs, source), restates Cross-Project Rule 9 (Cloud abstraction) with the full banned-terms list (`Kubernetes`, `cluster`, `BuildKit`, `ArgoCD`, `Cosign`, `Argo Rollouts`, `KEDA`, `Gitea`, `MinIO`, `ESO`, `cell`, `Pulumi`, `Helmfile`, `Tilt`, `Ory`, `Traefik`) and the allowed contexts (Architecture / Self-Hosted sections of `README.md`, this file, ADRs, internal runbooks, source). Mirrors the Locked Narrative governed by root `CLAUDE.md` (HERO, sub-headline, proof point, BODY anchor, hero pattern, demo interaction, positioning mix).

## [0.13.0] — 2026-04-09

### Added
- **GitHub App auto-deploy** — `git push` triggers automatic deploy (model Vercel)
  - GitHub App "LoopZero Platform" (ID 3319955) with HMAC-SHA256 webhook validation
  - Source download via GitHub API installation tokens
  - Tarball→git bundle conversion for build pipeline compatibility
  - S3 upload + build submission from webhook handler
  - Commit status lifecycle: pending on push, success/failure on build complete
  - `loopzero connect` / `loopzero disconnect` CLI commands (14 tests)
  - Repository linking API: POST/DELETE/GET `/api/v1/projects/{name}/connect`
  - DB: `github_installations` + `project_repositories` tables (migration 027)
- **Custom Domains v2** — Cloudflare Zone Management (model Vercel)
  - `loopzero domain add` creates CF zone per domain, scans DNS, imports records
  - User only changes nameservers — CF handles TLS + WAF/DDoS
  - Apex domains via CF CNAME flattening (no A record workaround needed)
  - `loopzero domain dns` command for DNS record management
  - DB: `cf_zone_id`, `cf_zone_status`, `cf_nameservers` columns (migration 026)
  - State machine: `pending_ns` → `ns_active` → `provisioning` → `active`
  - E2E validated with aquim.ai (Python API + React frontend monorepo)
- **Monorepo Validator** — instant pre-upload validation
  - 30 framework recognition (Node, Go, Python, Ruby, Java, PHP, Rust)
  - Frontend auto-infers `render: static` (no explicit declaration needed)
  - Max 2 pods per project (frontends don't count)
  - Dockerfile detection for custom frameworks
  - Path traversal protection (SEC-10)
  - Validation runs in `loopzero deploy` before upload (<1s feedback)
  - 40 new tests covering all scenarios + bypass edge cases
- **Build/Release Garbage Collection** — prevents unbounded DB growth
  - Retain last 10 builds per project, delete older than 30 days
  - Hourly cleanup of builds table + events table
- **Manifest Validation Gate** — defense-in-depth for ArgoCD
  - Rejects Certificate/ClusterIssuer/Issuer CRs in gitops push path
  - Prevents ArgoCD sync blockage from stuck cert-manager resources
  - 4 unit tests

### Changed
- **Language support expanded** — 30 frameworks (was 18)
  - Added: `react`, `vue`, `svelte`, `astro`, `angular`, `fastify` (Node)
  - Added: `sinatra`, `rails`, `ruby` (Ruby)
  - Added: `spring`, `spring-boot`, `java` (Java)
  - Added: `laravel`, `slim`, `php` (PHP)
  - Added: `axum`, `actix`, `rocket`, `rust` (Rust)
  - loopzero-packs generates Dockerfiles for all new languages
  - Language strategies in argo/languages.go for build pipeline
- **Init container migration** — uses API image (not separate migrate image)
  - Migrations always in sync with running code (no ConfigMap desync)
  - `migrate` binary included in API Dockerfile (multi-stage)
- **Leader election** — retry loop (6 attempts, 30s) instead of single try
- **ClusterIssuer** — dual solver: DNS-01 for platform zone, HTTP-01 for custom domains
- **Dockerfile** — `go build ./cmd/` instead of `./cmd/main.go` (includes adapter files)

### Fixed
- **PG failover detection** — TCP keepalive on pgx pool (KeepAlive=15s)
  - Detects dead connections in ~90s (was 2h Linux default)
  - Critical for leader election and advisory lock release
- **Leader heartbeat timeout** — 10s context instead of `context.Background()`
  - Prevents indefinite hang on zombie PG connection
- **Non-leader re-election** — periodic retry (60s) for advisory lock
  - Pods no longer stay non-leader forever after PG recovery
- **Commit status SHA** — uses `build.CommitSHA` (source) not `release.GitCommit` (gitops)
  - Was returning 422 "No commit found" because gitops SHA doesn't exist on GitHub
- **ArgoCD revision mismatch** — retrigger sync instead of silent skip
  - Convergence loop now forces re-sync when revision doesn't match
- **Framework mapping gap** — CLI recognized 17 frameworks, server only 11
  - All CLI-recognized frameworks now mapped server-side
- **CI pipeline** — fixed 33 Go lint issues, 2 CLI lint errors, go mod tidy
- **loopzero-stacks templates** — all 19 templates fixed (removed `commands`, `render: server`, worker ports)

### Security
- **Manifest validation gate** — Certificate CRs blocked from gitops tree
- **Path traversal protection** — SEC-10 in CLI validator
- **GitHub webhook HMAC-SHA256** — validates all incoming events
- **Commit status permissions** — `statuses:write` on GitHub App installation

## [0.12.0] — 2026-04-08

### Added
- **Plan Enforcement** — Starter/Pro/Team limits enforced on projects/builds/environments/members
- **Audit Log** — mutation events written to DB, GET /api/v1/audit endpoint, `loopzero audit` CLI command

## [0.11.0] — 2026-04-06

### Added
- **Cloud Auth System** — full authentication deployed on DigitalOcean
  - Login via GitHub, Google (OIDC), and email/password
  - User registration with email verification (6-digit code via Resend)
  - Ory Kratos session-based auth (cookie, no tokens in browser)
  - Ory Hydra for CLI Device Code Flow (RFC 8628)
  - Dashboard at `cloud.dev.loopzero.example` with profile, logout, verification banner
  - Verification page auto-submits code from email link
- **Cloud Service** (`cloud/`) — Go service for OAuth2 consent, billing, metering
  - Device Code Flow endpoints (`/v1/auth/device/*`)
  - User info endpoint (`/v1/auth/me`) with JWT claims from Kratos identity
  - Stripe billing integration (webhook, subscriptions, plan limits)
  - Consent handler for Hydra login/consent flows
- **Dashboard** (`cloud/dashboard/`) — React SPA (Vite + Tailwind)
  - Login, register, verification, profile pages
  - Overview, projects, environments, team, billing, settings pages
  - Session-based auth guard (redirects to login when unauthenticated)
  - Verification banner for unverified email users
- **Email delivery via Resend HTTP API** — Kratos HTTP courier
  - DigitalOcean blocks SMTP ports; uses `delivery_strategy: http`
  - Jsonnet template maps `ctx.recipient/subject/html_body` to Resend API
  - Requires `--watch-courier` flag on Kratos
- **Security hardening** (MEET-033)
  - Password: min 10 chars + HaveIBeenPwned breach check
  - Cloudflare WAF rate-limiting: 10 login/min, 5 register/min per IP
  - Account enumeration mitigation (`security.account_enumeration.mitigate: true`)
- **Content hash deploy** — deploy without git (MEET-028)
  - Safe tar extraction (Go `archive/tar`, no path traversal/symlinks)
  - 30 new tests for content hash functions
- **ArgoCD SYNC_FAILED fix** — 7 root causes resolved (MEET-029)
  - Callback auth header, leader election hardening, timeout hierarchy
  - TriggerSync 404 recovery, convergence loop revision check
- **CI/CD pipeline chain** — push → CI → Build Images → Infra Deploy
  - Wait-for-CI polling in Build Images
  - Wait-for-images polling in Infra Deploy
  - cloud-api + dashboard added to build pipeline
- **API Gateway** — Traefik path-based routing under `cloud.dev.loopzero.example`
  - `/self-service/*` → Kratos, `/oauth2/*` → Hydra, `/auth/*` → cloud service
  - CORS middleware on loopzero-api for cross-origin dashboard calls

- DNS A records: `cloud.dev.loopzero.example`, `auth.dev.loopzero.example` (#MEET-033)

  - Meeting record with 6 specialist agents (Architect, DistSys, SRE, GitOps, API Domain, Verification)
  - ADR-0007: ArgoCD Application model evaluation (per-project vs notification scoping)

  - 7 new Prometheus metrics: drift_detected, release_state_divergence, releases_pending,
    reconcile_loop_last_success_timestamp, gitops_operation_duration, release_convergence_duration,
    releasing_builds (was registered, now has PrometheusRule)

- Workload protection Phase 1 + Phase 2: defense against broken/unhealthy container images (#MEET-021)
  - Startup probe on all server/frontend apps (150s max startup, 5s period, 3s timeout)
  - Readiness probe with thresholds (10s period, 3 failures before removal from Service)
  - Liveness probe with thresholds (15s period, 5 failures — more tolerant than readiness)
  - progressDeadlineSeconds: 210s (fail-fast broken deploys, 40s margin over startup)
  - minReadySeconds: 10s (stabilization window for quick crashes)
  - Rolling update strategy: maxUnavailable=0, maxSurge=1 (zero-downtime deploys)
  - PodDisruptionBudget for ALL tenant workloads (server + worker + frontend)
  - unhealthyPodEvictionPolicy: AlwaysAllow (broken pods never block node drain)
  - terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 30s (explicit graceful shutdown contract)
  - 6 new PrometheusRules: CrashLoop (5m), ImagePullFailed, ReplicaMismatch (5m),
    HighRestartRate, DeployStuck (ProgressDeadlineExceeded), CrashLoopCritical (30m)
  - 4 ValidatingAdmissionPolicies (K8s native, no Kyverno): restrict registries,
    disallow :latest, require resource limits, protect platform NetworkPolicies
  - Build retry skips OOM (exit 137) via retryStrategy.expression (Argo Workflows native)

- `CircuitBreakerWorkflowSubmitter` — wraps `WorkflowSubmitter` with circuit breaker for `Submit` operations. Opens after 3 consecutive Argo API failures, closes after 2 recovery probes. `Cancel` and `GetStatus` bypass the breaker (best-effort/read-only). Prevents 70s of wasted retries when build cluster is down (#REVIEW-MOD-5)

- `ReleaseReaper` background goroutine — finds releases stuck in READY (>5min) or PENDING (>ArgoCD timeout) and transitions them to GIT_MIRROR_FAILED or SYNC_FAILED with SSE notification (#PIPELINE-RELEASE-REAPER)
